# Phase 8: Disaster Recovery (Recovery Services Vaults, Blob Vaulted Backup, Backup Alert Routing, Operator-Run Failover) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make a prod West US 3 regional outage recoverable within **RTO 1 hour / RPO 15 minutes**, operator-driven from one runbook:
- one Recovery Services vault per stamp (geo-redundant, cross-region restore, reversible soft delete and immutability), with the jump host's daily Enhanced backup where it exists;
- blob vaulted backup of the prod application container in a Backup vault;
- Azure Backup alerts routed to the Phase 6 action group;
- an operator-run `failover.bicep` that brings the East US warm standby to primary strength, and runbook 08 for failover, failback, restores, quarterly drills and the irreversible hardening after the first drill.

**Architecture:**
- **Pipeline side (`main.bicep`, `deploy.yml`).**
  - `modules/recoveryServicesVault.bicep` is deployed by every stamp (`rsv-defenstack-<env>-<code>`). It has `vaultstorageconfig` (GeoRedundant, cross-region restore) and diagnostics to the central workspace, and is locked in prod. Only in the stamp with the jump host (`enableVirtualMachine && isPrimary`) does it add the policy `vm-daily-enhanced` and the protected item.
  - `modules/blobBackupVault.bicep` is deployed only where `enableBlobVaultedBackup` (default prod primary). It creates `bv-defenstack-prod-wus3`, the policy `blob-daily-vaulted`, Storage Account Backup Contributor for the vault on the app account, and a backup instance for `def-blob`.
  - `modules/backupAlertRouting.bicep` (`apr-defenstack-<env>-backup`) sits in the global resource group next to the action group, scoped to the regional resource groups.
- **Operator side (never the pipeline).** `failover.bicep` (subscription scope, repository root) with `params/failover.bicepparam`, prod East US only, armed by `failoverActive`. It deploys four modules into `rg-defenstack-prod-eus`:
  - the App Service plan scaled to 3 (`modules/failover/appServicePlanScale.bicep`);
  - Bastion (`modules/bastion.bicep`);
  - a second Azure Monitor private endpoint `ampls-defenstack-prod-eus-pe` (`modules/monitorPrivateEndpoint.bicep`, which gains an optional `privateEndpointName`);
  - a blob private endpoint `<account>-eus-pe` to the primary account (`modules/failover/storagePrivateEndpoint.bicep`).

  `deploy.yml` ignores the failover-only paths. CI builds, lints and PSRules it through the test suite and `-InputPath params/`. `failover.json` is committed and drift-tested, like `main.json` and `governance.json` (ADR-032).
- **Docs.** Runbook 08 (9 sections), ADR-030 to ADR-033, and updates to runbooks 00b, 01, 05, 06 and 07, the overview, the README and `docs/cost.md`.

**Tech Stack:** Bicep CLI 0.47.16, Azure CLI 2.90+, Windows PowerShell 5.1 / pwsh 7, Pester 5.x, PSRule 2.9.0 with PSRule.Rules.Azure 1.47.0, GitHub Actions.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-secure-connectivity-design.md`. The relevant parts are §3 "Data and application resilience" (the Recovery Services vault per region), the "Backup job failure" alert of §3 "Monitoring, detection and response", §5 Phase 8 (row 8), §6 documentation standard and the §7 DR drill.

This plan builds on Phase 7 (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-04-phase7-governance.md`). The branch `phase8-dr` is stacked on `phase7-governance` (commit `2759d16`).

**Verification status of this plan:** every Bicep module, parameter file, test, workflow and document below was prototyped and run before the plan was written. The reference commits are local branches, not for merge: `phase8-proto-t1` … `phase8-proto-t4` (the state after Tasks 1–4) and `phase8-prototype` (the state after Task 5).
- `bicep lint` and `bicep build` are clean for every file.
- `main.json`, `governance.json` and `failover.json` are in sync.
- The full Pester suite passes: 687 → **711** (Task 1) → **731** (Task 2) → **739** (Task 3) → **783** (Task 4) → **810** (Task 5).
- `Assert-PSRule` reports 0 failures and 0 errors: 1332 → **1368** rules processed (Task 1) → **1378** (Task 2) → **1394** (Task 3) → **1448** (Task 4 and Task 5). PSRule needs `SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS` set (see Execution Notes). One new exclusion, `Azure.RSV.ReplicationAlert`, is justified by ADR-030.
- `deploy.yml` parses as YAML.
- The prototype ran on Windows PowerShell 5.1 only, because pwsh 7 was not installed. The new tests use no 7-only syntax, but run them on pwsh 7 too (see Global Constraints).

The embedded content is that verified content. Transcribe it exactly.

## Global Constraints

- **User decisions (binding, taken when this plan was written):**
  - **RTO 1 hour / RPO 15 minutes** for a prod West US 3 regional outage. Failover is **operator-driven from runbook 08**. Runbook 08 §1 states how each is met: Front Door's priority origins and the RA-GZRS secondary for serving, the Last Sync Time for the RPO. Write availability waits for the storage failover and is measured in every drill. 15 minutes is Microsoft's expectation, not an SLA (ADR-031).
  - **The data tier fails over.** Runbook 08 includes the customer-managed storage account failover to East US (unplanned, `az storage account failover --failover-type Unplanned`, then re-enable geo-redundancy), and the planned failback.
  - **Vault immutability starts enabled and Unlocked.** Locking it, and always-on soft delete, are **irreversible runbook 08 §5.3 steps taken only after the first successful drill**, with an ADR-030 sign-off (date, approver, drill row). Until then, both vaults keep soft delete `Enabled`/`On` for 14 days (never `AlwaysON`/`AlwaysOn`) and `immutabilitySettings.state: 'Unlocked'`. The hardening is a reviewed Bicep change deployed by the pipeline; the CLI is only a fallback (a CLI-only lock would make the next deploy try to unlock it).
  - **Blob vaulted backup** of the prod app storage account is in scope, in addition to the spec's Recovery Services vaults.
- **Approved design (binding):**
  - Recovery Services vault per stamp: `rsv-defenstack-<env>-<code>` (dev West US 3, prod West US 3, prod East US). `RS0`/Standard, system-assigned identity, `publicNetworkAccess: 'Disabled'` with **no private endpoint** (ADR-033). Soft delete `softDeleteState: 'Enabled'`, 14 days, `enhancedSecurityState: 'Enabled'`. `monitoringSettings.azureMonitorAlertSettings.alertsForAllJobFailures: 'Enabled'`, with both classic alert settings `'Disabled'`. Child `vaultstorageconfig`: `storageModelType: 'GeoRedundant'`, `crossRegionRestoreFlag: true`; the protected item depends on it, because it locks once an item is registered. Diagnostic setting `rsv-diagnostics`, `Dedicated`, six resource-specific categories (no `AddonAzureBackupAlerts`). Lock `<vault>-lck` in prod.
  - VM backup, only where `enableVirtualMachine && isPrimary`: policy `vm-daily-enhanced` (`AzureIaasVM`, `V2`, daily 02:00 UTC, 7 days instant restore, 30 daily and 12 weekly Sunday). The protected item is named `Azure/iaasvmcontainer;iaasvmcontainerv2;<rg>;<vm>/vm;iaasvmcontainerv2;<rg>;<vm>`, with the VM name and resource group parsed from the VM ID.
  - Backup vault `bv-defenstack-<env>-<code>`, only where `enableBlobVaultedBackup` (stamp param, default `environmentName == 'prod' && regionRole == 'primary'`). System-assigned identity only. VaultStore GeoRedundant; soft delete `On` 14 days; immutability Unlocked; cross-region restore `Disabled` (unsupported for blobs); job-failure alerts on. Diagnostic setting `bv-diagnostics` (four categories). Lock in prod. Policy `blob-daily-vaulted`: Discrete VaultStore backup `R/2026-01-01T02:00:00+00:00/P1D` in UTC, kept `P30D`. Storage Account Backup Contributor (`e5e2a7ff-d759-4cd2-bb51-3152d37e2eb1`) is granted to the vault on the app account only, as `guid(storageAccount.id, vault.id, roleId)`. Backup instance named after the account, `containersList: ['def-blob']` (from `storage.outputs.blobContainerName`), depending on the role assignment.
  - Alert processing rule `apr-defenstack-<env>-backup` in `rg-defenstack-<env>-global`: `location: 'global'`, no `kind`. Scopes are the primary resource group, plus the secondary with DR. Condition `MonitorService Equals 'Azure Backup'`; action `AddActionGroups` with the Phase 6 action group. It does not wait for the stamps.
  - `failover.bicep`: `environmentName` `@allowed(['prod'])`, `location` `@allowed(['eastus'])`, `appServicePlanCapacity = 3` (1–30), `primaryStorageAccountId` (default computed as `main.bicep` and `regionStamp.bicep` name the prod primary account) and `failoverActive = true`. `failoverActive` gates all four modules; false deploys nothing. The modules are `failover-app-service-plan-eus`, `failover-bastion-eus`, `failover-monitor-private-endpoint-eus` and `failover-storage-private-endpoint-eus`, all in `rg-defenstack-prod-eus`. **Nothing targets West US 3**: the region appears only in the default primary account ID, which is used only as the blob endpoint target and its name. **No data role in Bicep**: the DR write grant stays a manual, PIM-gated runbook step (ADR-020).
- **Operator-run failover and the pipeline freeze (ADR-032):** `failover.bicep` is deployed only by an operator with `az deployment sub create` during a declared outage (runbook 08 §4 step 5). `main.bicep` and `deploy.yml` never reference it, and `deploy.yml` ignores `modules/failover/**` and `params/failover.bicepparam`. **From the declaration until failback step 9, the pipeline is frozen** (`gh workflow disable deploy`, no merges to `main`): a pipeline run would fail in West US 3 or undo the failover (plan back to 1 instance, Azure Monitor records back to the dead endpoint). Failback re-enables it for one prod dispatch at §5.2 step 4.
- **Pipeline identity:** Storage Account Backup Contributor `e5e2a7ff-d759-4cd2-bb51-3152d37e2eb1` joins the default `-DelegatableRoleDefinitionIds` of `scripts/New-GitHubDeploymentIdentity.ps1`; the ABAC condition is built from that list. **The operator re-runs the identity script before the first Phase 8 deploy** (runbook 00b), or the role assignment is refused. No other identity change; `failover.bicep` runs under the operator's own PIM roles.
- **Spec values (verbatim):**
  - §3: "**Recovery Services vault** per region: GRS, cross-region restore, soft delete, immutability. Covers management VMs." The prototype puts one vault in every stamp, so dev gets one too.
  - §3 alerts: "Backup job failure."
  - §5 row 8: "Recovery Services vaults, hub↔hub peering, DR enablement flag", doc `08-dr-failover.md` ("**failover and failback step by step, RTO/RPO, drill log**"). The hub↔hub peering already shipped in Phase 6 (ADR-022). The DR enablement flag is `deploySecondaryAdminAccess` (Phase 3) for the steady state, and `failover.bicep`'s `failoverActive` for a declared outage.
  - §7: "**DR drill:** disable the primary origin in Front Door; traffic serves from East US. Record the observed RTO in `08-dr-failover.md`."
- **New API versions:**
  - `Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults@2026-07-01`, with `vaults/backupPolicies` and `vaults/backupFabrics/protectionContainers/protectedItems` on the same version
  - `Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/backupstorageconfig@2026-07-01`. **Deviation:** the design named `@2026-08-01`, but Bicep 0.47.16 has no types for it (BCP081). 2026-07-01 is the newest typed version and carries the same two properties.
  - `Microsoft.DataProtection/backupVaults@2026-06-01`, with `backupPolicies` and `backupInstances` on the same version
  - `Microsoft.AlertsManagement/actionRules@2021-08-08` (GA)
  - `Microsoft.Web/serverfarms@2025-03-01` (the `appService.bicep` version)
  - Existing versions for role assignments (`2022-04-01`), locks (`2020-05-01`), diagnostic settings (`2021-05-01-preview`, ADR-025), private endpoints (`2024-07-01`), storage (`2026-04-01`, `existing`), VNets (`2025-09-01`), ASGs (`2024-07-01`), workspace (`2026-03-01`) and AMPLS (`2021-09-01`).
  - `vaults/backupFabrics` has no Bicep types at any version, so the protected item takes its full slash name rather than a parent chain.
  - The backup instance's `objectType: 'BlobBackupDatasourceParameters'` (the REST guide's type for explicit containers) is not in the Bicep types and carries `#disable-next-line BCP036` (ADR-031). Do not "correct" it to `AdlsBlobBackupDatasourceParameters`, which is for Data Lake (HNS) accounts; that is the live fallback only.
- **PSRule:** one new exclusion, `Azure.RSV.ReplicationAlert`, in `ps-rule.yaml` with the comment `# ADR-030: no Site Recovery replication exists; backup alerts are Azure Monitor alerts routed to the action group.` (a test pins it, and ADR-030 justifies it). The East US plan's `Azure.AppService.AvailabilityZone` is covered by the existing ADR-008 suppression `DefenStack.SingleInstanceAppServicePlans`. The VM backup policy and protected item are not in PSRule's expansion, because both parameter files keep `enableVirtualMachine = false`.
- **`SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS`:** every PSRule run sets the placeholder `security-contact@example.com` (Phase 7, because `params/` holds `governance.bicepparam`). Without it, `Azure.Defender.SecurityContact` fails. `bicep-ci.yml` already sets it.
- **Unchanged:** `governance.bicep`, `params/governance.bicepparam`, `params/dev.bicepparam`, `params/prod.bicepparam`, `bicep-ci.yml`, `.gitignore` (already `modules/**/*.json`), `storage.bicep` (it already enables versioning and change feed and manages no object replication policy), `appService.bicep`, `bastion.bicep`, every Phase 1–7 resource name, regions and address plan. The Phase 6 Azure Monitor endpoint keeps its name `ampls-defenstack-<env>-pe` through the new parameter's default.
- **main.json, governance.json and failover.json are generated:** after any `.bicep` change (including modules), run `bicep build main.bicep`, `bicep build governance.bicep` and, from Task 4, `bicep build failover.bicep`, and commit whichever changed. Check with `diff --strip-trailing-cr <(bicep build main.bicep --stdout) main.json`, and the same for `governance` and `failover`. `tests/Lint.Tests.ps1` checks all three. Phase 8 does not change `governance.json`: `bicep build governance.bicep` leaves a line-ending-only diff, so restore it with `git checkout -- governance.json`.
- **Docs are mandatory:** runbooks use the 9-section template. Runbook 08 cites Microsoft Learn for platform behaviour and lists each unverified item as a live-only risk with a fallback.
- **Tests must run on both shells:** Windows PowerShell 5.1 and pwsh 7. The prototype ran on 5.1 only; run the full suite on pwsh 7 too before the final commit and report the count.
- **No live Azure/Entra/GitHub commands; no push.** Nothing in this plan calls Azure; runbook 08 is for a human after the PR.
- **Commit trailers:** every commit ends with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- **Build output:** never commit `modules/**/*.json` (the `.gitignore` already covers `modules/failover/*.json`).
- **PowerShell editing gotchas:**
  - Never write Bicep through a double-quoted PowerShell string, because `${...}` is expanded and dropped.
  - In single-quoted strings, `''` is one `'`.
  - A helper `.ps1` containing non-ASCII characters (—, →, ↔, §) needs a UTF-8 BOM.
  - Read and write docs as UTF-8.
  - The working tree is CRLF (`core.autocrlf=true`): normalize line endings before matching a find text, or a multi-line find silently misses. Files written fresh are LF in the working tree; Git's "LF will be replaced by CRLF" warnings are expected.
  - Windows PowerShell 5.1 returns a JSON array from `ConvertFrom-Json` as one object; use `@(...)[0]`.

## Review Focus

These are inputs and conditions the spec implies but no offline test can fully exercise, most likely first:

1. **ARM rejects the blob vaulted backup** on its first deploy: the datasource object type, the role assignment racing the backup instance, or the account's disabled public access. Pinned by the Task 2 tests "selects containers with BlobBackupDatasourceParameters, the documented type for blob vaulted backup (Adls… is for Data Lake accounts)", "creates the instance after the role assignment, which it needs to set up object replication" and "grants the vault Storage Account Backup Contributor on the application account only, with a deterministic name", and the Task 5 test "the DR runbook lists each unverified item as a live-only risk with a fallback".
2. **The pipeline identity cannot assign the backup role**, because the ABAC condition lacks Storage Account Backup Contributor or the operator skipped the identity script re-run. Pinned by the Task 2 test "constrains RBAC Administrator with the exact ABAC condition for the delegatable roles (Storage Blob Data Contributor, Virtual Machine Administrator Login, Storage Blob Data Reader, Monitoring Metrics Publisher, Storage Account Backup Contributor)" and the Task 5 test "runbook 00b adds the Storage Account Backup Contributor role and asks for an identity script re-run before Phase 8".
3. **A vault setting becomes irreversible too early, or locks before it is right:** storage redundancy written after the first protected item, AlwaysON soft delete or a Locked immutability before the first drill. Pinned by the Task 1 tests "sets storage before any protected item, since it locks once an item is registered", "keeps soft delete Enabled for 14 days, never the irreversible AlwaysON (ADR-030)" and "starts immutability Unlocked; locking is a runbook 08 step after the first drill (ADR-030)", and the Task 5 test "the DR runbook has the quarterly drill, the drill log table and the irreversible hardening with an ADR sign-off".
4. **The failover writes to West US 3, or the pipeline undoes it:** `failover.bicep` scopes a deployment into the dead region, or a failover-only commit (or any run during DR) redeploys the workload and scales East US back to 1. Pinned by the Task 4 tests "targets nothing in West US 3: the region appears only in the default primary storage account ID", "uses the West US 3 account ID only as the blob endpoint target and its name, never as a deployment scope" and "does not redeploy the workload when only failover files change (a redeploy scales the East US plan back to 1, ADR-032)", and the Task 5 test "the DR runbook freezes the pipeline during DR".
5. **Backup alerts reach no one**, because the rule's scope misses a vault or matches the wrong service. Pinned by the Task 3 tests "matches only Azure Backup alerts", "scopes the rule to the regional resource groups that hold the vaults, the secondary only with DR" and "does not wait for the stamps: the resource groups exist before any vault (runbook 01)", and the Task 5 test "the DR runbook has backup alert triage through the alert processing rule".

Known coverage gaps:
- PSRule does not evaluate the VM backup policy or the protected item (`enableVirtualMachine = false` in both parameter files). The prototype checked them once with a scratch parameter file (prod with the jump host); do not commit such a file.
- Container discovery on the protected item's first PUT, the RSV with public access denied and no private endpoint, the global resource group's reachability during a West US 3 outage, private link after the workspace switchover, and a new blob endpoint connection while the account is failing over are all live-only (runbook 08 §9).
- The `az backup vault backup-properties set` and `az dataprotection backup-vault update` flag names in runbook 08 §5.3, and `az backup recoverypoint list --use-secondary-region`, come from the research, not a live call; the runbook says to check them with `--help`.
- End-to-end alert routing is proven only by the drill's test alert (runbook 08 §5.3 step 5).

## Execution Notes

- **Full suite:** `powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File tests/Invoke-Tests.ps1`, then `pwsh -NoProfile -File tests/Invoke-Tests.ps1`. Each prints `Tests Passed: N, Failed: 0`. It takes about 6 minutes.
- **Some files only:**

  ```powershell
  powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command "& { `$f = @('tests/RecoveryServicesVault.Tests.ps1'); & ./tests/Invoke-Tests.ps1 -Path `$f }"
  ```

- **PSRule** (the `SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS` placeholder is required):

  ```powershell
  powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command "$env:PSRULE_AZURE_BICEP_PATH = (Get-Command bicep).Source; $env:SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS = 'security-contact@example.com'; Assert-PSRule -InputPath params/ -Module PSRule.Rules.Azure -Format File -Outcome Fail, Error"
  ```

  The summary line reports the rules processed; the expected count is given per task.
- **Build the entry points:** `bicep build main.bicep`, `bicep build governance.bicep` (then `git checkout -- governance.json` if only line endings changed) and, from Task 4, `bicep build failover.bicep`.
- **Lint:** `for f in main.bicep governance.bicep failover.bicep modules/*.bicep modules/governance/*.bicep modules/failover/*.bicep; do [ -f "$f" ] && { bicep lint "$f" || echo "LINT FAIL $f"; }; done`
- **YAML check:** `powershell.exe -NoProfile -Command "Import-Module powershell-yaml; foreach (`$f in '.github/workflows/deploy.yml', '.github/workflows/bicep-ci.yml') { ConvertFrom-Yaml (Get-Content `$f -Raw) | Out-Null }; 'YAML-OK'"`

### Task 1: Recovery Services vault per stamp and the jump host's backup

**Files:**
- Create: `modules/recoveryServicesVault.bicep`, `tests/RecoveryServicesVault.Tests.ps1`
- Replace: `modules/regionStamp.bicep`, `ps-rule.yaml`, `tests/RegionStamp.Tests.ps1`, `tests/VirtualMachine.Tests.ps1`
- Modify: `main.json` (regenerate)

**Interfaces:**
- **Consumes (Phases 1–7):** `regionStamp.bicep`'s `names` object, `location`, `logAnalyticsWorkspaceId`, `isProd`, `isPrimary` and its VM condition `enableVirtualMachine && isPrimary`; `virtualMachine.bicep`'s existing `output id`; the repository's diagnostic-setting (`2021-05-01-preview`, ADR-025) and lock (`enableDeleteLock`, `<name>-lck`) patterns; `ps-rule.yaml`'s `rule.exclude` list.
- **Produces:**
  - `recoveryServicesVault.bicep(location, vaultName, logAnalyticsWorkspaceId, virtualMachineId = '', enableDeleteLock = false)`, outputs `id` and `name`; variables `protectVirtualMachine`, `policyName` (`vm-daily-enhanced`), `protectionContainerName`, `protectedItemName`, `backupLogCategories` and `dailyRunTime`.
  - `regionStamp.bicep`: `names.recoveryServicesVault` (`rsv-defenstack-<env>-<code>`), module `recovery-services-vault` in every stamp, outputs `recoveryServicesVaultId` and `recoveryServicesVaultName`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Confirm the branch**

  The worktree `.claude/worktrees/phase8-dr` is on branch `phase8-dr` at `2759d16` plus this plan's commit. Check it with `git log --oneline -2`. The baseline is `Tests Passed: 687, Failed: 0` and 1332 PSRule rules processed (with `SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS` set).

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

  Create `tests/RecoveryServicesVault.Tests.ps1`:

  ```powershell
  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force
      $template = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/recoveryServicesVault.bicep'
      $vault = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults' | Select-Object -First 1
      $storageConfig = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/backupstorageconfig' | Select-Object -First 1
      $policy = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/backupPolicies' | Select-Object -First 1
      $protectedItem = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/backupFabrics/protectionContainers/protectedItems' | Select-Object -First 1
      $diagnostics = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/diagnosticSettings' | Select-Object -First 1
      $lock = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Authorization/locks' | Select-Object -First 1
  }

  Describe 'Recovery Services vault (Phase 8)' {
      It 'pins the GA API version 2026-07-01 with the Standard SKU and a system-assigned identity' {
          $vault.apiVersion | Should -Be '2026-07-01'
          $vault.sku.name | Should -Be 'RS0'
          $vault.sku.tier | Should -Be 'Standard'
          $vault.identity.type | Should -Be 'SystemAssigned'
      }

      It 'denies public network access with no private endpoint; Azure VM backup does not need one (ADR-033)' {
          $vault.properties.publicNetworkAccess | Should -Be 'Disabled'
          Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints' | Should -BeNullOrEmpty
      }

      It 'keeps soft delete Enabled for 14 days, never the irreversible AlwaysON (ADR-030)' {
          $softDelete = $vault.properties.securitySettings.softDeleteSettings
          $softDelete.softDeleteState | Should -BeExactly 'Enabled'
          $softDelete.softDeleteRetentionPeriodInDays | Should -Be 14
          $softDelete.enhancedSecurityState | Should -BeExactly 'Enabled'
          ($vault.properties | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 20) | Should -Not -Match 'AlwaysON'
      }

      It 'starts immutability Unlocked; locking is a runbook 08 step after the first drill (ADR-030)' {
          $vault.properties.securitySettings.immutabilitySettings.state | Should -BeExactly 'Unlocked'
      }

      It 'raises Azure Monitor alerts for every job failure and turns the classic alerts off' {
          $monitoring = $vault.properties.monitoringSettings
          $monitoring.azureMonitorAlertSettings.alertsForAllJobFailures | Should -Be 'Enabled'
          $monitoring.classicAlertSettings.alertsForCriticalOperations | Should -Be 'Disabled'
          $monitoring.classicAlertSettings.emailNotificationsForSiteRecovery | Should -Be 'Disabled'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Recovery Services vault storage (Phase 8)' {
      It 'stores backups geo-redundantly with cross-region restore, as the fixed child vaultstorageconfig' {
          $storageConfig.name | Should -Match "'vaultstorageconfig'\)\]$"
          $storageConfig.apiVersion | Should -Be '2026-07-01'
          $storageConfig.properties.storageModelType | Should -Be 'GeoRedundant'
          $storageConfig.properties.crossRegionRestoreFlag | Should -BeExactly $true
      }

      It 'sets storage before any protected item, since it locks once an item is registered' {
          @($protectedItem.dependsOn) | Should -Contain "[resourceId('Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/backupstorageconfig', parameters('vaultName'), 'vaultstorageconfig')]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Jump host backup (Phase 8)' {
      It 'protects nothing by default, so stamps without the jump host get an empty vault' {
          $template.parameters.virtualMachineId.defaultValue | Should -BeExactly ''
          $template.variables.protectVirtualMachine | Should -Be "[not(empty(parameters('virtualMachineId')))]"
          $policy.condition | Should -Be "[variables('protectVirtualMachine')]"
          $protectedItem.condition | Should -Be "[variables('protectVirtualMachine')]"
      }

      It 'uses an Enhanced (V2) Azure VM policy in UTC, which Trusted Launch VMs require' {
          $policy.properties.backupManagementType | Should -Be 'AzureIaasVM'
          $policy.properties.policyType | Should -Be 'V2'
          $policy.properties.timeZone | Should -Be 'UTC'
      }

      It 'backs up daily at 02:00 UTC and keeps 7 days of instant-restore snapshots' {
          $schedule = $policy.properties.schedulePolicy
          $schedule.schedulePolicyType | Should -Be 'SimpleSchedulePolicyV2'
          $schedule.scheduleRunFrequency | Should -Be 'Daily'
          $template.variables.dailyRunTime | Should -Be '2026-01-01T02:00:00Z'
          @($schedule.dailySchedule.scheduleRunTimes) | Should -Be @("[variables('dailyRunTime')]")
          @($policy.properties.retentionPolicy.dailySchedule.retentionTimes) | Should -Be @("[variables('dailyRunTime')]")
          @($policy.properties.retentionPolicy.weeklySchedule.retentionTimes) | Should -Be @("[variables('dailyRunTime')]")
          $policy.properties.instantRpRetentionRangeInDays | Should -Be 7
      }

      It 'keeps 30 daily and 12 weekly (Sunday) recovery points' {
          $retention = $policy.properties.retentionPolicy
          $retention.retentionPolicyType | Should -Be 'LongTermRetentionPolicy'
          $retention.dailySchedule.retentionDuration.count | Should -Be 30
          $retention.dailySchedule.retentionDuration.durationType | Should -Be 'Days'
          $retention.weeklySchedule.retentionDuration.count | Should -Be 12
          $retention.weeklySchedule.retentionDuration.durationType | Should -Be 'Weeks'
          @($retention.weeklySchedule.daysOfTheWeek) | Should -Be @('Sunday')
      }

      It 'names the container and item iaasvmcontainerv2;<rg>;<vm> under the Azure fabric' {
          $template.variables.protectionContainerName | Should -Be "[format('iaasvmcontainer;iaasvmcontainerv2;{0};{1}', variables('virtualMachineResourceGroupName'), variables('virtualMachineName'))]"
          $template.variables.protectedItemName | Should -Be "[format('vm;iaasvmcontainerv2;{0};{1}', variables('virtualMachineResourceGroupName'), variables('virtualMachineName'))]"
          $protectedItem.name | Should -Be "[format('{0}/Azure/{1}/{2}', parameters('vaultName'), variables('protectionContainerName'), variables('protectedItemName'))]"
      }

      It 'reads the VM name and resource group from the VM resource ID' {
          $template.variables.virtualMachineName | Should -Be "[last(split(parameters('virtualMachineId'), '/'))]"
          $template.variables.virtualMachineResourceGroupName | Should -Be "[if(empty(parameters('virtualMachineId')), '', split(parameters('virtualMachineId'), '/')[4])]"
      }

      It 'protects the VM with the policy' {
          $protectedItem.properties.protectedItemType | Should -Be 'Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines'
          $protectedItem.properties.sourceResourceId | Should -Be "[parameters('virtualMachineId')]"
          $protectedItem.properties.policyId | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/backupPolicies', parameters('vaultName'), variables('policyName'))]"
          @($protectedItem.dependsOn) | Should -Contain "[resourceId('Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/backupPolicies', parameters('vaultName'), variables('policyName'))]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Recovery Services vault operations (Phase 8)' {
      It 'sends the resource-specific backup tables to the central workspace, without the classic alert table' {
          $diagnostics.properties.workspaceId | Should -Be "[parameters('logAnalyticsWorkspaceId')]"
          $diagnostics.properties.logAnalyticsDestinationType | Should -Be 'Dedicated'
          $diagnostics.properties.copy[0].count | Should -Be "[length(variables('backupLogCategories'))]"
          @($template.variables.backupLogCategories) -join ',' |
              Should -Be 'CoreAzureBackup,AddonAzureBackupJobs,AddonAzureBackupPolicy,AddonAzureBackupStorage,AddonAzureBackupProtectedInstance,AzureBackupOperations'
      }

      It 'locks the vault only when requested' {
          $template.parameters.enableDeleteLock.defaultValue | Should -BeExactly $false
          $lock.condition | Should -Be "[parameters('enableDeleteLock')]"
          $lock.properties.level | Should -Be 'CanNotDelete'
          $lock.name | Should -Be "[format('{0}-lck', parameters('vaultName'))]"
      }

      It 'outputs the vault ID and name for the alert processing rule and runbook 08' {
          $template.outputs.id.value | Should -Match 'Microsoft\.RecoveryServices/vaults'
          $template.outputs.name.value | Should -Be "[parameters('vaultName')]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Recovery Services vault PSRule exclusion (Phase 8)' {
      It 'excludes only the Site Recovery replication-alert rule, citing ADR-030; Azure Backup alerts go through Azure Monitor' {
          $text = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'ps-rule.yaml') -Raw
          $text | Should -Match '# ADR-030:[^\r\n]*\r?\n\s*- Azure\.RSV\.ReplicationAlert'
          Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.RecoveryServices/vaults/replicationAlertSettings' | Should -BeNullOrEmpty
      }
  }
  ```

  Replace `tests/RegionStamp.Tests.ps1` (adds `Region stamp backup (Phase 8)` at the end):

  ```powershell
  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force
      $stamp = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/regionStamp.bicep'
      function Get-StampModuleParameters([string]$Name) {
          (Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name $Name).properties.parameters
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp naming' {
      It 'names every resource with environment and region so stamps never collide' {
          foreach ($key in 'hubVnet', 'spokeVnet', 'firewall', 'firewallPolicy', 'firewallPublicIp', 'appServicePlan', 'appService') {
              $stamp.variables.names.$key | Should -Match "parameters\('environmentName'\), parameters\('regionCode'\)"
          }
      }

      It 'derives globally unique names from subscription, environment, and region' {
          $stamp.variables.nameSuffix | Should -Be "[uniqueString(subscription().id, parameters('environmentName'), parameters('location'))]"
          $stamp.variables.names.storageAccount | Should -Be "[format('st{0}{1}', parameters('regionCode'), variables('nameSuffix'))]"
          $stamp.variables.names.keyVault | Should -Be "[format('kv-{0}-{1}', parameters('regionCode'), variables('nameSuffix'))]"
      }

      It 'passes the stamp names to the modules' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'app-service').appServicePlanName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').appServicePlan]"
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').firewallName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').firewall]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp availability' {
      It 'deploys the firewall and its public IP across zones 1-3 in every stamp' {
          @($stamp.variables.availabilityZones) -join ',' | Should -Be '1,2,3'
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').availabilityZones.value | Should -Be "[variables('availabilityZones')]"
      }

      It 'makes the App Service plan zone-redundant with 3 instances only in the prod primary region' {
          $parameters = Get-StampModuleParameters 'app-service'
          $parameters.zoneRedundant.value | Should -Be "[and(variables('isProd'), variables('isPrimary'))]"
          $parameters.instanceCount | Should -Be "[if(and(variables('isProd'), variables('isPrimary')), createObject('value', 3), createObject('value', 1))]"
      }

      It 'uses RA-GZRS in the prod primary region, GRS in the warm standby and LRS in dev (Phase 5)' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'storage').storageAccountSkuName |
              Should -Be "[if(variables('isProd'), if(variables('isPrimary'), createObject('value', 'Standard_RAGZRS'), createObject('value', 'Standard_GRS')), createObject('value', 'Standard_LRS'))]"
      }

      It 'deploys the optional management VM in the primary region only' {
          (Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name 'virtual-machine').condition |
              Should -Be "[and(parameters('enableVirtualMachine'), variables('isPrimary'))]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp wiring carried from Phase 0' {
      It 'passes the admin source ranges (Bastion subnet, VPN pool, extra managementSourceCidrs) to <_> (F1, Phase 3)' -ForEach 'spoke-network', 'virtual-machine' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters $_).managementSourceCidrs.value | Should -Be "[variables('adminSourceCidrs')]"
      }

      It 'uses the address plan spoke range for both the spoke VNet and firewall sources (F5)' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').spokeAddressPrefixes.value | Should -Be "[parameters('addressPlan').spokeAddressSpace]"
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'spoke-network').vnetAddressSpace.value | Should -Be "[parameters('addressPlan').spokeAddressSpace]"
      }

      It 'derives private endpoint sources from the App Service and management subnet prefixes (F5)' {
          $value = (Get-StampModuleParameters 'spoke-network').approvedPrivateEndpointSourceCidrs.value
          $value | Should -Match "addressPlan'\)\.appServiceIntegrationSubnetPrefix"
          $value | Should -Match "addressPlan'\)\.managementSubnetPrefix"
          $value | Should -Match "addressPlan'\)\.vpnClientAddressPool"
          $value | Should -Match 'additionalPrivateEndpointSourceCidrs'
      }

      It 'uses firewall threat intelligence Deny in prod and Alert in dev (F9)' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').threatIntelMode |
              Should -Be "[if(variables('isProd'), createObject('value', 'Deny'), createObject('value', 'Alert'))]"
      }

      It 'enables Key Vault template deployment so az.getSecret() references resolve (F4)' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'key-vault').enabledForTemplateDeployment.value | Should -BeExactly $true
      }

      It 'passes the container name from the storage module output (F11)' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'network-integration').storageContainerName.value | Should -Match 'outputs.blobContainerName'
      }

      It 'registers private endpoints in the shared zones from the global layer' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'private-connectivity').privateDnsZoneIds.value | Should -Be "[parameters('privateDnsZoneIds')]"
      }

      It 'locks the spoke VNet in prod only' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'spoke-network').enableDeleteLock.value | Should -Be "[variables('isProd')]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp outputs' {
      It 'exposes the VNets the entry point links to the shared DNS zones' {
          foreach ($output in 'hubVnetName', 'hubVnetId', 'spokeVnetName', 'spokeVnetId', 'appServiceHostName') {
              $stamp.outputs.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Contain $output
          }
      }

      It 'outputs the private-endpoint subnet that hosts the AMPLS endpoint in the primary region (Phase 6)' {
          $stamp.outputs.privateEndpointSubnetId.value | Should -Be "[reference('spokeNetwork').outputs.privateEndpointSubnetId.value]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp firewall security (Phase 2)' {
      It 'deploys Azure Firewall Premium in every stamp' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').firewallTier.value | Should -Be 'Premium'
      }

      It 'runs IDPS in Deny in prod and Alert in dev' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').idpsMode |
              Should -Be "[if(variables('isProd'), createObject('value', 'Deny'), createObject('value', 'Alert'))]"
      }

      It 'limits OS update egress to the management subnet' {
          @((Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').managementAddressPrefixes.value) | Should -Be @("[parameters('addressPlan').managementSubnetPrefix]")
      }

      It 'locks the hub VNet, Key Vault and firewall resources in prod only (<_>)' -ForEach 'hub-network', 'key-vault', 'azure-firewall' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters $_).enableDeleteLock.value | Should -Be "[variables('isProd')]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp admin access (Phase 3)' {
      It 'derives admin sources from the Bastion subnet and VPN client pool, plus any extra managementSourceCidrs' {
          $stamp.variables.adminSourceCidrs |
              Should -Be "[concat(createArray(parameters('addressPlan').bastionSubnetPrefix, parameters('addressPlan').vpnClientAddressPool), parameters('managementSourceCidrs'))]"
      }

      It 'deploys <_> only when deployAdminAccess is true' -ForEach 'bastion', 'vpn-gateway' {
          (Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name $_).condition | Should -Be "[parameters('deployAdminAccess')]"
          $stamp.parameters.deployAdminAccess.defaultValue | Should -BeExactly $false
      }

      It 'uses VpnGw2AZ in prod and VpnGw1AZ in dev' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'vpn-gateway').skuName |
              Should -Be "[if(variables('isProd'), createObject('value', 'VpnGw2AZ'), createObject('value', 'VpnGw1AZ'))]"
      }

      It 'deploys <_> after the firewall, whose DNS proxy the hub uses' -ForEach 'bastion', 'vpn-gateway' {
          @((Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name $_).dependsOn) | Should -Contain 'azureFirewall'
      }

      It 'spreads <_> across zones 1-3' -ForEach 'bastion', 'vpn-gateway' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters $_).availabilityZones.value | Should -Be "[variables('availabilityZones')]"
      }

      It 'gives the gateway the region VPN client pool' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'vpn-gateway').vpnClientAddressPool.value | Should -Be "[parameters('addressPlan').vpnClientAddressPool]"
      }

      It 'routes GatewaySubnet spoke traffic to the first usable firewall address, computed before the firewall exists' {
          $stamp.variables.firewallPrivateIp | Should -Be "[cidrHost(parameters('addressPlan').firewallSubnetPrefix, 3)]"
          $hub = Get-StampModuleParameters 'hub-network'
          $hub.firewallPrivateIp.value | Should -Be "[variables('firewallPrivateIp')]"
          $hub.spokeAddressPrefixes.value | Should -Be "[parameters('addressPlan').spokeAddressSpace]"
          @((Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name 'hub-network').dependsOn) | Should -Not -Contain 'azureFirewall'
      }

      It 'lets Bastion reach only the management subnet' {
          @((Get-StampModuleParameters 'hub-network').bastionTargetAddressPrefixes.value) | Should -Be @("[parameters('addressPlan').managementSubnetPrefix]")
      }

      It 'passes the Bastion and gateway subnet prefixes from the address plan' {
          $hub = Get-StampModuleParameters 'hub-network'
          $hub.bastionSubnetAddressPrefix.value | Should -Be "[parameters('addressPlan').bastionSubnetPrefix]"
          $hub.gatewaySubnetAddressPrefix.value | Should -Be "[parameters('addressPlan').gatewaySubnetPrefix]"
      }

      It 'allows the VPN client pool through the firewall admin-access rules' {
          @((Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall').vpnClientAddressPrefixes.value) | Should -Be @("[parameters('addressPlan').vpnClientAddressPool]")
      }

      It 'turns on gateway transit only with admin access, after the gateway is provisioned' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'network-integration').useHubGateway.value | Should -Be "[parameters('deployAdminAccess')]"
          @((Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name 'network-integration').dependsOn) | Should -Contain 'vpnGateway'
      }

      It 'passes the admin group to the management VM' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'virtual-machine').adminGroupObjectId.value | Should -Be "[parameters('adminGroupObjectId')]"
      }

      It 'outputs the Bastion and gateway names (empty without admin access) and the expected firewall IP' {
          $stamp.outputs.bastionName.value | Should -Be "[if(parameters('deployAdminAccess'), variables('names').bastion, '')]"
          $stamp.outputs.vpnGatewayName.value | Should -Be "[if(parameters('deployAdminAccess'), variables('names').vpnGateway, '')]"
          $stamp.outputs.expectedFirewallPrivateIp.value | Should -Be "[variables('firewallPrivateIp')]"
      }

      It 'names Bastion and the gateway with environment and region' {
          foreach ($key in 'bastion', 'bastionPublicIp', 'vpnGateway', 'vpnGatewayPublicIp') {
              $stamp.variables.names.$key | Should -Match "parameters\('environmentName'\), parameters\('regionCode'\)"
          }
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp ingress output (Phase 4)' {
      It 'outputs the App Service resource ID for the Front Door Private Link origin' {
          $stamp.outputs.appServiceId.value | Should -Be "[reference('appService').outputs.appServiceAppId.value]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp hub peering (Phase 6)' {
      It 'takes an optional hub peering plan, absent by default (dev, or prod without DR)' {
          $stamp.parameters.hubPeering.nullable | Should -BeExactly $true
          $stamp.parameters.hubPeering.'$ref' | Should -Be '#/definitions/hubPeeringPlan'
      }

      It 'routes the other region''s spoke from AzureFirewallSubnet to the other firewall' {
          $hub = Get-StampModuleParameters 'hub-network'
          $hub.remoteSpokeAddressPrefixes.value | Should -Match "tryGet\(parameters\('hubPeering'\), 'remoteSpokeAddressPrefixes'\)"
          $hub.remoteFirewallPrivateIp.value | Should -Match "tryGet\(parameters\('hubPeering'\), 'remoteFirewallPrivateIp'\)"
      }

      It 'passes the Azure Monitor role and prefixes to the firewall rules' {
          $firewall = Get-StampModuleParameters 'azure-firewall'
          $firewall.monitorPeeringRole.value | Should -Be "[variables('monitorPeeringRole')]"
          $firewall.monitorClientPrefixes.value | Should -Match "tryGet\(parameters\('hubPeering'\), 'monitorClientPrefixes'\)"
          $firewall.monitorPrivateEndpointPrefixes.value | Should -Match "tryGet\(parameters\('hubPeering'\), 'monitorPrivateEndpointPrefixes'\)"
          $stamp.variables.monitorPeeringRole | Should -Match "tryGet\(parameters\('hubPeering'\), 'monitorRole'\), 'None'\)"
      }

      It 'lets the local firewall reach the AMPLS endpoint only in the region that hosts it (it SNATs peer traffic)' {
          $stamp.variables.peerTrafficSourceCidrs |
              Should -Be "[if(equals(variables('monitorPeeringRole'), 'Host'), createArray(parameters('addressPlan').firewallSubnetPrefix), createArray())]"
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'spoke-network').monitorPeerSourceCidrs.value | Should -Be "[variables('peerTrafficSourceCidrs')]"
      }

      It 'never adds the firewall subnet to the approved-https sources, which reach every private endpoint (final review I-1)' {
          $value = (Get-StampModuleParameters 'spoke-network').approvedPrivateEndpointSourceCidrs.value
          $value | Should -Not -Match 'peerTrafficSourceCidrs'
          $value | Should -Not -Match 'firewallSubnetPrefix'
      }

      It 'outputs the spoke''s AMPLS application security group for the endpoint (final review I-1)' {
          $stamp.outputs.monitorAsgId.value | Should -Be "[reference('spokeNetwork').outputs.amplsAsgId.value]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp jump host data collection endpoint (final review I-2)' {
      It 'outputs the jump host DCE ID when the VM is deployed in this stamp, else an empty string' {
          $stamp.outputs.dataCollectionEndpointId.value |
              Should -Be "[if(and(parameters('enableVirtualMachine'), variables('isPrimary')), reference('virtualMachine').outputs.dataCollectionEndpointId.value, '')]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp flow logs (Phase 6)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $flowLogs = Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name "[format('flow-logs-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), parameters('regionCode'))]"
      }

      It 'names the flow-log account and identity per environment and region' {
          $stamp.variables.names.flowLogStorageAccount | Should -Be "[format('stfl{0}{1}', parameters('regionCode'), variables('nameSuffix'))]"
          $stamp.variables.names.flowLogIdentity | Should -Match "parameters\('environmentName'\), parameters\('regionCode'\)"
      }

      It 'keeps flow logs zone-redundant in prod and locally redundant in dev' {
          (Get-StampModuleParameters 'flow-log-storage').storageAccountSkuName |
              Should -Be "[if(variables('isProd'), createObject('value', 'Standard_ZRS'), createObject('value', 'Standard_LRS'))]"
      }

      It 'deploys the flow logs into NetworkWatcherRG, where the region''s Network Watcher lives' {
          $flowLogs.resourceGroup | Should -Be 'NetworkWatcherRG'
          $flowLogs.properties.parameters.location.value | Should -Be "[parameters('location')]"
      }

      It 'logs both the hub and the spoke VNet' {
          $value = @($flowLogs.properties.parameters.virtualNetworks.value)
          $value[0].name | Should -Be "[variables('names').hubVnet]"
          $value[0].id | Should -Be "[reference('hubNetwork').outputs.id.value]"
          $value[1].name | Should -Be "[variables('names').spokeVnet]"
          $value[1].id | Should -Be "[reference('spokeNetwork').outputs.id.value]"
      }

      It 'writes with the flow-log identity to the flow-log account and analyses into the central workspace' {
          $parameters = $flowLogs.properties.parameters
          $parameters.storageAccountId.value | Should -Be "[reference('flowLogStorage').outputs.id.value]"
          $parameters.identityId.value | Should -Be "[reference('flowLogStorage').outputs.identityId.value]"
          $parameters.logAnalyticsWorkspaceId.value | Should -Be "[parameters('logAnalyticsWorkspaceId')]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp alerts (Phase 6)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $alertParameters = Get-StampModuleParameters 'metric-alerts'
          $rules = @($alertParameters.rules.value)
      }

      It 'names the alerts per environment and region and notifies the shared action group' {
          $alertParameters.namePrefix.value | Should -Be "[format('alert-defenstack-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), parameters('regionCode'))]"
          $alertParameters.actionGroupId.value | Should -Be "[parameters('actionGroupId')]"
          $stamp.parameters.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Contain 'alertThresholds'
      }

      It 'defines exactly the seven regional rules' {
          @($rules | ForEach-Object { $_.name }) -join ',' |
              Should -Be 'firewall-health,firewall-snat-ports,app-http-5xx,app-health-check,key-vault-availability,key-vault-forbidden,vpn-p2s-connections'
      }

      It 'alerts on <Metric> <Operator> <Threshold> (<Aggregation>) at Sev<Severity> as <Name>' -ForEach @(
          @{ Name = 'firewall-health'; Metric = 'FirewallHealth'; Namespace = 'Microsoft.Network/azureFirewalls'; Operator = 'LessThan'; Threshold = '90'; Aggregation = 'Average'; Severity = 1; Resource = "[reference('azureFirewall').outputs.id.value]" }
          @{ Name = 'firewall-snat-ports'; Metric = 'SNATPortUtilization'; Namespace = 'Microsoft.Network/azureFirewalls'; Operator = 'GreaterThan'; Threshold = '80'; Aggregation = 'Maximum'; Severity = 2; Resource = "[reference('azureFirewall').outputs.id.value]" }
          @{ Name = 'app-http-5xx'; Metric = 'Http5xx'; Namespace = 'Microsoft.Web/sites'; Operator = 'GreaterThan'; Threshold = "[string(parameters('alertThresholds').appServiceHttp5xx)]"; Aggregation = 'Total'; Severity = 2; Resource = "[reference('appService').outputs.appServiceAppId.value]" }
          @{ Name = 'app-health-check'; Metric = 'HealthCheckStatus'; Namespace = 'Microsoft.Web/sites'; Operator = 'LessThan'; Threshold = '100'; Aggregation = 'Average'; Severity = 1; Resource = "[reference('appService').outputs.appServiceAppId.value]" }
          @{ Name = 'key-vault-availability'; Metric = 'Availability'; Namespace = 'Microsoft.KeyVault/vaults'; Operator = 'LessThan'; Threshold = '99.9'; Aggregation = 'Average'; Severity = 2; Resource = "[reference('keyVault').outputs.id.value]" }
          @{ Name = 'key-vault-forbidden'; Metric = 'ServiceApiResult'; Namespace = 'Microsoft.KeyVault/vaults'; Operator = 'GreaterThan'; Threshold = "[string(parameters('alertThresholds').keyVaultForbidden)]"; Aggregation = 'Count'; Severity = 3; Resource = "[reference('keyVault').outputs.id.value]" }
          @{ Name = 'vpn-p2s-connections'; Metric = 'P2SConnectionCount'; Namespace = 'Microsoft.Network/virtualNetworkGateways'; Operator = 'GreaterThan'; Threshold = "[string(parameters('alertThresholds').vpnP2sConnections)]"; Aggregation = 'Maximum'; Severity = 3; Resource = "[if(parameters('deployAdminAccess'), reference('vpnGateway').outputs.id.value, '')]" }
      ) {
          $rule = $rules | Where-Object { $_.name -eq $Name }
          $rule | Should -Not -BeNullOrEmpty
          $rule.metricName | Should -BeExactly $Metric
          $rule.metricNamespace | Should -BeExactly $Namespace
          $rule.operator | Should -Be $Operator
          $rule.threshold | Should -Be $Threshold
          $rule.timeAggregation | Should -Be $Aggregation
          $rule.severity | Should -Be $Severity
          $rule.resourceId | Should -Be $Resource
          $rule.description | Should -Not -BeNullOrEmpty
      }

      It 'counts only Key Vault results with status code 403' {
          $rule = $rules | Where-Object { $_.name -eq 'key-vault-forbidden' }
          @($rule.dimensions).Count | Should -Be 1
          $rule.dimensions[0].name | Should -Be 'StatusCode'
          $rule.dimensions[0].operator | Should -Be 'Include'
          @($rule.dimensions[0].values) | Should -Be @('403')
      }

      It 'creates the VPN alert only where the gateway exists' {
          ($rules | Where-Object { $_.name -eq 'vpn-p2s-connections' }).deploy | Should -Be "[parameters('deployAdminAccess')]"
          foreach ($rule in $rules | Where-Object { $_.name -ne 'vpn-p2s-connections' }) {
              $rule.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'deploy' -Because $rule.name
          }
      }
  }

  Describe 'Region stamp backup (Phase 8)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $vaultModule = Get-ModuleDeployment -Template $stamp -Name 'recovery-services-vault'
          $vaultParameters = $vaultModule.properties.parameters
      }

      It 'deploys one Recovery Services vault in every stamp, named rsv-defenstack-<env>-<region>' {
          $vaultModule.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'condition'
          $stamp.variables.names.recoveryServicesVault | Should -Be "[format('rsv-defenstack-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), parameters('regionCode'))]"
          $vaultParameters.vaultName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').recoveryServicesVault]"
          $vaultParameters.location.value | Should -Be "[parameters('location')]"
          $vaultParameters.logAnalyticsWorkspaceId.value | Should -Be "[parameters('logAnalyticsWorkspaceId')]"
      }

      It 'locks the vault in prod only' {
          $vaultParameters.enableDeleteLock.value | Should -Be "[variables('isProd')]"
      }

      It 'protects the jump host only in the stamp where it exists' {
          $vaultParameters.virtualMachineId |
              Should -Be "[if(and(parameters('enableVirtualMachine'), variables('isPrimary')), createObject('value', reference('virtualMachine').outputs.id.value), createObject('value', ''))]"
      }

      It 'outputs the vault ID and name for the alert processing rule and runbook 08' {
          $stamp.outputs.recoveryServicesVaultId.value | Should -Be "[reference('recoveryServicesVault').outputs.id.value]"
          $stamp.outputs.recoveryServicesVaultName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').recoveryServicesVault]"
      }
  }
  ```

  Replace `tests/VirtualMachine.Tests.ps1` (adds `VM backup hand-off (Phase 8)` at the end):

  ```powershell
  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force
      $template = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/virtualMachine.bicep'
  }

  Describe 'VM NIC NSG management access (F1)' {
      It 'exposes managementSourceCidrs defaulting to an empty list' {
          $template.parameters.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Contain 'managementSourceCidrs'
          @($template.parameters.managementSourceCidrs.defaultValue).Count | Should -Be 0
      }
  }

  Describe 'Jump host just-in-time access (Phase 7, ADR-028)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $nsg = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Network/networkSecurityGroups' | Select-Object -First 1
          $nic = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Network/networkInterfaces' | Select-Object -First 1
          $jit = @(Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Security/locations/jitNetworkAccessPolicies')
          $inboundRules = @($nsg.properties.securityRules | Where-Object { $_ -isnot [string] -and $_.properties.direction -eq 'Inbound' })
          $denyRule = $template.variables.denyManagementInboundRule
      }

      It 'has no static allow rule on the NIC: SSH and RDP open only through a JIT grant' {
          ($nsg.properties.securityRules | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10) | Should -Not -Match "'Allow'|""Allow"""
          ($nsg.properties.securityRules | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10) | Should -Not -Match 'allow-management-ssh-rdp'
          $template.variables.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'managementInboundRules'
      }

      It 'denies inbound SSH and RDP from any source at priority 4000, leaving 100-3999 free for JIT allow rules' {
          @($nsg.properties.securityRules)[0] | Should -Be "[variables('denyManagementInboundRule')]"
          $denyRule.name | Should -Be 'deny-ssh-rdp-inbound'
          $denyRule.properties.priority | Should -Be 4000
          $denyRule.properties.access | Should -Be 'Deny'
          $denyRule.properties.direction | Should -Be 'Inbound'
          $denyRule.properties.sourceAddressPrefix | Should -Be '*'
          $denyRule.properties.protocol | Should -Be '*'
          @($denyRule.properties.destinationPortRanges) -join ',' | Should -Be '22,3389'
      }

      It 'denies exactly the ports the JIT policy opens' {
          @($denyRule.properties.destinationPortRanges) -join ',' | Should -Be (@($template.variables.jitPorts | ForEach-Object { [string]$_ }) -join ',')
      }

      It 'keeps the catch-all inbound deny below the SSH/RDP deny' {
          $inboundRules.Count | Should -Be 1
          $inboundRules[0].name | Should -Be 'deny-unsolicited-inbound'
          $inboundRules[0].properties.priority | Should -Be 4096
      }

      It 'attaches the NSG to the NIC, the NSG closest to the VM, which JIT programs' {
          $nic.properties.networkSecurityGroup.id | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.Network/networkSecurityGroups', variables('networkSecurityGroupName'))]"
      }

      It 'creates one JIT policy, named default in the VM region, with the GA API and kind Basic' {
          $jit.Count | Should -Be 1
          $jit[0].apiVersion | Should -Be '2020-01-01'
          $jit[0].name | Should -Be "[format('{0}/default', parameters('location'))]"
          $jit[0].kind | Should -Be 'Basic'
      }

      It 'skips the JIT policy when no admin source is given, so the ports stay closed' {
          $jit[0].condition | Should -Be "[not(empty(parameters('managementSourceCidrs')))]"
      }

      It 'covers this VM on TCP 22 and 3389, from the admin sources only, for at most three hours' {
          $vmEntry = @($jit[0].properties.virtualMachines)
          $vmEntry.Count | Should -Be 1
          $vmEntry[0].id | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines', parameters('vmName'))]"
          @($template.variables.jitPorts) -join ',' | Should -Be '22,3389'
          $ports = $vmEntry[0].copy[0]
          $ports.name | Should -Be 'ports'
          $ports.count | Should -Be "[length(variables('jitPorts'))]"
          $ports.input.number | Should -Be "[variables('jitPorts')[copyIndex('ports')]]"
          $ports.input.protocol | Should -Be 'TCP'
          $ports.input.allowedSourceAddressPrefixes | Should -Be "[parameters('managementSourceCidrs')]"
          $ports.input.maxRequestAccessDuration | Should -Be 'PT3H'
      }
  }

  Describe 'VM monitoring (F3)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $diagnostics = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/diagnosticSettings' | Select-Object -First 1
          $dcr = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/dataCollectionRules' | Select-Object -First 1
          $association = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/dataCollectionRuleAssociations' | Select-Object -First 1
          $agent = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines/extensions' | Select-Object -First 1
      }

      It 'sends only metrics through the VM diagnostic setting (VMs expose no log categories)' {
          $diagnostics.properties.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'logs'
          $diagnostics.properties.metrics[0].category | Should -Be 'AllMetrics'
      }

      It 'installs the Azure Monitor Agent' {
          $agent.properties.publisher | Should -Be 'Microsoft.Azure.Monitor'
          $agent.properties.enableAutomaticUpgrade | Should -BeTrue
      }

      It 'sends guest logs and performance counters to the workspace through a DCR' {
          $dcr.properties.destinations.logAnalytics[0].workspaceResourceId | Should -Be "[parameters('logAnalyticsWorkspaceId')]"
      }

      It 'associates the DCR with the VM' {
          $association.scope | Should -Match 'virtualMachines'
      }

      It 'outputs the DCR ID' {
          $template.outputs.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Contain 'dataCollectionRuleId'
      }

      It 'uses Azure Monitor counter paths with a leading backslash' {
          # dataSources compiles to the expression '[union(variables(...), variables(...))]' rather than
          # an inline object, so the literal counter text lives in the performanceCounterSource variable.
          $dcrText = $template.variables.performanceCounterSource | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10
          $dcrText | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('\\Processor(*)\\% Processor Time'))
      }
  }

  Describe 'VM data collection endpoint in the AMPLS (final review I-2)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $endpoints = @(Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/dataCollectionEndpoints')
          $associations = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/dataCollectionRuleAssociations'
          $ruleAssociation = $associations | Where-Object { $_.name -eq "[format('{0}-dcra', parameters('vmName'))]" }
          $configurationAssociation = $associations | Where-Object { $_.name -eq 'configurationAccessEndpoint' }
      }

      It 'creates one regional data collection endpoint, named after the VM, with public network access disabled' {
          $endpoints.Count | Should -Be 1
          $endpoints[0].apiVersion | Should -Be '2023-03-11'
          $endpoints[0].name | Should -Be "[format('{0}-dce', parameters('vmName'))]"
          $endpoints[0].location | Should -Be "[parameters('location')]"
          $endpoints[0].properties.networkAcls.publicNetworkAccess | Should -Be 'Disabled'
      }

      It 'associates the endpoint with the VM as its configurationAccessEndpoint' {
          @($configurationAssociation).Count | Should -Be 1
          $configurationAssociation.apiVersion | Should -Be '2023-03-11'
          $configurationAssociation.scope | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines', parameters('vmName'))]"
          $configurationAssociation.properties.dataCollectionEndpointId | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.Insights/dataCollectionEndpoints', format('{0}-dce', parameters('vmName')))]"
          $configurationAssociation.properties.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'dataCollectionRuleId'
      }

      It 'keeps the existing DCR association unchanged' {
          $associations.Count | Should -Be 2
          @($ruleAssociation).Count | Should -Be 1
          $ruleAssociation.properties.dataCollectionRuleId | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.Insights/dataCollectionRules', variables('dataCollectionRuleName'))]"
          $ruleAssociation.properties.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'dataCollectionEndpointId'
      }

      It 'outputs the data collection endpoint ID' {
          $template.outputs.dataCollectionEndpointId.value | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.Insights/dataCollectionEndpoints', format('{0}-dce', parameters('vmName')))]"
      }
  }

  Describe 'VM monitoring on Windows (F3)' {
      It 'installs the Windows agent and collects System and Application events when osType is Windows' {
          $template.variables.azureMonitorAgentName | Should -Match "'AzureMonitorWindowsAgent'"
          $template.variables.osLogSource | Should -Match 'windowsEventLogs'
          $template.variables.osLogSource | Should -Match 'Microsoft-Event'
          $template.variables.osLogSource | Should -Match 'Application!'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Jump host access (Phase 3)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $vm = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines' | Select-Object -First 1
          $extensions = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines/extensions'
          $entraLogin = $extensions | Where-Object { $_.properties.publisher -eq 'Microsoft.Azure.ActiveDirectory' }
          $roleAssignment = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Authorization/roleAssignments' | Select-Object -First 1
          $nsg = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Network/networkSecurityGroups' | Select-Object -First 1
      }

      It 'pins the VM to one availability zone, zone 1 by default' {
          @($vm.zones) | Should -Be @("[parameters('availabilityZone')]")
          $template.parameters.availabilityZone.defaultValue | Should -Be '1'
      }

      It 'installs the Entra ID login extension that matches the OS' {
          $template.variables.entraLoginExtensionName | Should -Be "[if(equals(parameters('osType'), 'Linux'), 'AADSSHLoginForLinux', 'AADLoginForWindows')]"
          $entraLogin.properties.type | Should -Be "[variables('entraLoginExtensionName')]"
      }

      It 'installs the Entra extension after the Azure Monitor Agent (one extension operation at a time)' {
          ($entraLogin.dependsOn -join ' ') | Should -Match 'azureMonitorAgentName'
      }

      It 'grants the admin group Virtual Machine Administrator Login on this VM only, when a group is given' {
          $roleAssignment.condition | Should -Be "[not(empty(parameters('adminGroupObjectId')))]"
          $roleAssignment.scope | Should -Match 'virtualMachines'
          $roleAssignment.properties.principalType | Should -Be 'Group'
          $template.variables.virtualMachineAdministratorLoginRoleId | Should -Be '1c0163c0-47e6-4577-8991-ea5c82e286e4'
          $template.parameters.adminGroupObjectId.defaultValue | Should -Be ''
      }

      It 'hands patching to Update Manager on both operating systems' {
          $template.variables.patchSettings.patchMode | Should -Be 'AutomaticByPlatform'
          $template.variables.patchSettings.assessmentMode | Should -Be 'AutomaticByPlatform'
          $template.variables.patchSettings.automaticByPlatformSettings.bypassPlatformSafetyChecksOnUserSchedule | Should -BeExactly $true
          # osProfile compiles to one if() expression; both the Linux and Windows branches carry the settings.
          ([regex]::Matches($vm.properties.osProfile, [regex]::Escape("'patchSettings', variables('patchSettings')"))).Count | Should -Be 2
      }

      It 'installs critical and security updates in a weekly window and assigns it to the VM' {
          $schedule = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Maintenance/maintenanceConfigurations' | Select-Object -First 1
          $assignment = Get-TemplateResource -Template $template -Type 'Microsoft.Maintenance/configurationAssignments' | Select-Object -First 1
          $schedule.properties.maintenanceScope | Should -Be 'InGuestPatch'
          $schedule.properties.maintenanceWindow.recurEvery | Should -Be 'Week Sunday'
          @($schedule.properties.installPatches.linuxParameters.classificationsToInclude) -join ',' | Should -Be 'Critical,Security'
          @($schedule.properties.installPatches.windowsParameters.classificationsToInclude) -join ',' | Should -Be 'Critical,Security'
          $assignment.scope | Should -Match 'virtualMachines'
      }

      It 'denies outbound SSH and RDP from the NIC (PSRule Azure.NSG.LateralTraversal)' {
          ($nsg.properties.securityRules | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 10) | Should -Match 'deny-ssh-rdp-outbound'
      }
  }

  Describe 'VM backup hand-off (Phase 8)' {
      It 'outputs the VM resource ID, which the Recovery Services vault protects' {
          $template.outputs.id.value | Should -Be "[resourceId('Microsoft.Compute/virtualMachines', parameters('vmName'))]"
      }
  }
  ```
