### Task 4: Operator-run `failover.bicep` for the East US warm standby

**Files:**
- Create: `failover.bicep`, `params/failover.bicepparam`, `modules/failover/appServicePlanScale.bicep`, `modules/failover/storagePrivateEndpoint.bicep`, `tests/Failover.Tests.ps1`
- Replace: `modules/monitorPrivateEndpoint.bicep`, `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`, `tests/Lint.Tests.ps1`, `tests/MonitorPrivateLink.Tests.ps1`, `tests/Params.Tests.ps1`, `tests/Workflows.Tests.ps1`
- Modify: `failover.json` (generate), `main.json` (regenerate)

**Interfaces:**
- **Consumes (Phases 1–7):**
  - `main.bicep`'s `regionCodes` (`eus`), `privateDnsZoneNames`, the primary account name formula (`primaryLocation` westus3, `primaryResourceGroupName`) and the global resource group `rg-defenstack-<env>-global` with `log-defenstack-<env>` and `ampls-defenstack-<env>`.
  - `regionStamp.bicep`'s names in the East US stamp: `asp-…-eus`, `bas-…-eus`, `pip-bas-…-eus` (already in `governance.bicep`'s public IP allowlist), the hub and spoke VNets with `AzureBastionSubnet` and `private-endpoints`, and `…-spoke-ampls-asg`.
  - `appService.bicep`'s prod SKU (`P2V3`/`PremiumV3`) and plan API version; `bastion.bicep` as-is; `monitorPrivateEndpoint.bicep`; `privateConnectivity.bicep`'s storage endpoint shape (connection and group `blob`, zone group `default`, config `blob`).
  - The `Generated ARM template` drift test, the `params/*.bicepparam` build test and the `deploy.yml` path filters.
- **Produces:**
  - `failover.bicep` (`targetScope = 'subscription'`): params `environmentName = 'prod'`, `location = 'eastus'`, `appServicePlanCapacity = 3`, `primaryStorageAccountId` and `failoverActive = true`; variables `regionCode`, `resourceGroupName`, `globalResourceGroupName`, `privateDnsZoneNames` and `names`; outputs `resourceGroupName`, `appServicePlanName`, `bastionName`, `monitorPrivateEndpointId` and `storagePrivateEndpointId` (empty when disarmed).
  - `appServicePlanScale.bicep(location, appServicePlanName, capacity)`, output `name`; `storagePrivateEndpoint.bicep(location, storageAccountId, privateEndpointName, privateEndpointSubnetId, blobPrivateDnsZoneId)`, outputs `id` and `name`.
  - `monitorPrivateEndpoint.bicep` param `privateEndpointName = '${privateLinkScopeName}-pe'`.
  - `params/failover.bicepparam` with `using '../failover.bicep'`, and `primaryStorageAccountId` left to the template default.
  - `deploy.yml` path filters `'!modules/failover/**'` and `'!params/failover.bicepparam'`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

  Create `tests/Failover.Tests.ps1`:

  ```powershell
  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force
      $failover = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'failover.bicep'
      $main = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'main.bicep'
      $planTemplate = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/failover/appServicePlanScale.bicep'
      $plan = Get-TemplateResource -Template $planTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Web/serverfarms' | Select-Object -First 1
      $appServiceTemplate = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/appService.bicep'
      $deployments = @(Get-TemplateResource -Template $failover -Type 'Microsoft.Resources/deployments')
      [Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
      $failoverJson = (& bicep build (Get-RepoPath 'failover.bicep') --stdout) -join "`n"

      # Module names are expressions here, so find each deployment by its name prefix.
      function Get-FailoverModule([string]$Prefix) {
          $found = @($deployments | Where-Object { $_.name.StartsWith("[format('$Prefix-{0}'") })
          if ($found.Count -ne 1) { throw "Expected one '$Prefix' deployment, found $($found.Count)." }
          $found[0]
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover entry point (Phase 8)' {
      It 'is a subscription-scope template at the repository root' {
          Get-RepoPath 'failover.bicep' | Should -Exist
          $failover.'$schema' | Should -Match 'subscriptionDeploymentTemplate\.json'
      }

      It 'is never deployed by main.bicep or the pipeline (operator only during a declared outage, ADR-032)' {
          Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'main.bicep') -Raw | Should -Not -Match 'failover\.bicep'
          $deploy = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath '.github/workflows/deploy.yml') -Raw
          $deploy | Should -Not -Match 'failover\.bicep\b'
      }

      It 'is armed by failoverActive, true by default, and every module waits on it' {
          $failover.parameters.failoverActive.type | Should -Be 'bool'
          $failover.parameters.failoverActive.defaultValue | Should -BeTrue
          $deployments.Count | Should -Be 4
          foreach ($deployment in $deployments) {
              $deployment.condition | Should -Be "[parameters('failoverActive')]" -Because $deployment.name
          }
      }

      It 'serves prod in East US only' {
          @($failover.parameters.environmentName.allowedValues) | Should -Be @('prod')
          @($failover.parameters.location.allowedValues) | Should -Be @('eastus')
          $failover.variables.regionCode | Should -Be 'eus'
          $failover.variables.resourceGroupName | Should -Be "[format('rg-defenstack-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
          $failover.variables.globalResourceGroupName | Should -Be "[format('rg-defenstack-{0}-global', parameters('environmentName'))]"
      }

      It 'deploys every module into the East US resource group, and nothing else at all' {
          @($failover.resources).Count | Should -Be $deployments.Count
          foreach ($deployment in $deployments) {
              $deployment.resourceGroup | Should -Be "[variables('resourceGroupName')]" -Because $deployment.name
              $deployment.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'subscriptionId' -Because $deployment.name
              $deployment.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'scope' -Because $deployment.name
          }
      }

      It 'targets nothing in West US 3: the region appears only in the default primary storage account ID' {
          $withoutAccountId = $failoverJson | ConvertFrom-Json
          $withoutAccountId.parameters.PSObject.Properties.Remove('primaryStorageAccountId')
          $rest = $withoutAccountId | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100
          $rest | Should -Not -Match 'wus3'
          $rest | Should -Not -Match 'westus3'
          $failover.parameters.primaryStorageAccountId.defaultValue | Should -Match "^\[resourceId\(subscription\(\)\.subscriptionId, .*'Microsoft\.Storage/storageAccounts'"
      }

      It 'uses the West US 3 account ID only as the blob endpoint target and its name, never as a deployment scope' {
          $references = @($deployments | Where-Object { [regex]::Unescape(($_.properties.parameters | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 100 -Compress)) -match "parameters\('primaryStorageAccountId'\)" })
          $references.Count | Should -Be 1
          $references[0].name | Should -Match "^\[format\('failover-storage-private-endpoint-\{0\}'"
          $references[0].properties.parameters.storageAccountId.value | Should -Be "[parameters('primaryStorageAccountId')]"
          $references[0].resourceGroup | Should -Be "[variables('resourceGroupName')]"
          $failover.variables.names.storagePrivateEndpoint | Should -Match "parameters\('primaryStorageAccountId'\)"
          $endpoint = Get-TemplateResource -Template $references[0].properties.template -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints' | Select-Object -First 1
          $endpoint.properties.privateLinkServiceConnections[0].properties.privateLinkServiceId | Should -Be "[parameters('storageAccountId')]"
      }

      It 'names the shared zones exactly as main.bicep does, so the endpoint re-points the live records' {
          $expected = $main.variables.privateDnsZoneNames
          $actual = $failover.variables.privateDnsZoneNames
          foreach ($key in 'blob', 'sites', 'vault', 'monitor', 'oms', 'ods', 'agentsvc') {
              $actual.$key | Should -BeExactly $expected.$key -Because $key
          }
      }

      It 'uses the same region code as main.bicep' {
          $main.variables.regionCodes.eastus | Should -Be $failover.variables.regionCode
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover: App Service plan scale-out (Phase 8)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $module = Get-FailoverModule 'failover-app-service-plan'
      }

      It 'scales to the prod primary capacity, 3 by default' {
          $failover.parameters.appServicePlanCapacity.defaultValue | Should -Be 3
          $failover.parameters.appServicePlanCapacity.minValue | Should -Be 1
          $failover.parameters.appServicePlanCapacity.maxValue | Should -Be 30
          $module.properties.parameters.capacity.value | Should -Be "[parameters('appServicePlanCapacity')]"
      }

      It 'patches the existing East US plan by name' {
          $module.properties.parameters.appServicePlanName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').appServicePlan]"
          $failover.variables.names.appServicePlan | Should -Be "[format('asp-defenstack-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
          $module.properties.parameters.location.value | Should -Be "[parameters('location')]"
      }

      It 'keeps the prod SKU that appService.bicep gives every prod plan (P2V3, PremiumV3)' {
          $appServiceTemplate.variables.appServicePlanSkuName | Should -Match "'prod'\), 'P2V3'"
          $appServiceTemplate.variables.appServicePlanSkuTier | Should -Match "'prod'\), 'PremiumV3'"
          $plan.sku.name | Should -Be 'P2V3'
          $plan.sku.tier | Should -Be 'PremiumV3'
          $plan.sku.capacity | Should -Be "[parameters('capacity')]"
      }

      It 'uses the plan API version of appService.bicep and leaves the warm-standby plan zonal settings unchanged' {
          $appServicePlan = Get-TemplateResource -Template $appServiceTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Web/serverfarms' | Select-Object -First 1
          $plan.apiVersion | Should -Be $appServicePlan.apiVersion
          $plan.properties.zoneRedundant | Should -BeExactly $false
          @($plan.properties.PSObject.Properties.Name) | Should -Be @('zoneRedundant')
      }

      It 'touches only the plan, never the application' {
          @(Get-TemplateResource -Template $planTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Web/sites').Count | Should -Be 0
          $planTemplate.parameters.capacity.minValue | Should -Be 1
          $planTemplate.parameters.capacity.maxValue | Should -Be 30
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover: East US Bastion (Phase 8)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $module = Get-FailoverModule 'failover-bastion'
      }

      It 'reuses modules/bastion.bicep with the stamp names the governance allowlist already permits' {
          $module.properties.parameters.bastionName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').bastion]"
          $failover.variables.names.bastion | Should -Be "[format('bas-defenstack-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
          $module.properties.parameters.publicIpName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').bastionPublicIp]"
          $failover.variables.names.bastionPublicIp | Should -Be "[format('pip-bas-defenstack-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
          @(Get-TemplateResource -Template $module.properties.template -Type 'Microsoft.Network/bastionHosts').Count | Should -Be 1
      }

      It 'places Bastion in the existing AzureBastionSubnet of the East US hub' {
          $module.properties.parameters.subnetId.value | Should -Match "Microsoft\.Network/virtualNetworks/subnets"
          $module.properties.parameters.subnetId.value | Should -Match "'AzureBastionSubnet'"
          $module.properties.parameters.subnetId.value | Should -Match "variables\('resourceGroupName'\)"
          $module.properties.parameters.subnetId.value | Should -Match "variables\('names'\)\.hubVnet"
          $failover.variables.names.hubVnet | Should -Be "[format('vnet-defenstack-{0}-{1}-hub', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
      }

      It 'spreads Bastion across three zones, as the stamp does, and logs to the shared workspace' {
          @($module.properties.parameters.availabilityZones.value) | Should -Be @('1', '2', '3')
          $module.properties.parameters.logAnalyticsWorkspaceId.value | Should -Match "Microsoft\.OperationalInsights/workspaces"
          $module.properties.parameters.logAnalyticsWorkspaceId.value | Should -Match "format\('log-defenstack-\{0\}'"
      }

      It 'produces a public IP name that governance.bicep allows for prod East US' {
          [Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
          $governance = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'governance.bicep'
          ($governance.functions[0].members.publicIpNames.output.value -join "`n") | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("format('pip-bas-defenstack-{0}-{1}'"))
          @($governance.parameters.environmentRegions.defaultValue.prod) | Should -Contain 'eastus'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover: East US Azure Monitor private endpoint (Phase 8)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $module = Get-FailoverModule 'failover-monitor-private-endpoint'
      }

      It 'reuses modules/monitorPrivateEndpoint.bicep with a region-specific endpoint name' {
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').monitorPrivateEndpoint]"
          $failover.variables.names.monitorPrivateEndpoint | Should -Be "[format('ampls-defenstack-{0}-{1}-pe', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
          $module.properties.parameters.location.value | Should -Be "[parameters('location')]"
      }

      It 'connects to the existing environment scope in the global resource group' {
          $module.properties.parameters.privateLinkScopeName.value | Should -Be "[format('ampls-defenstack-{0}', parameters('environmentName'))]"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateLinkScopeId.value | Should -Match "Microsoft\.Insights/privateLinkScopes"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateLinkScopeId.value | Should -Match "variables\('globalResourceGroupName'\)"
      }

      It 'sits in the East US spoke private-endpoint subnet and joins its AMPLS application security group' {
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointSubnetId.value | Should -Match "'private-endpoints'"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointSubnetId.value | Should -Match "variables\('resourceGroupName'\)"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointSubnetId.value | Should -Match "variables\('names'\)\.spokeVnet"
          $failover.variables.names.spokeVnet | Should -Be "[format('vnet-defenstack-{0}-{1}-spoke', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
          $module.properties.parameters.applicationSecurityGroupId.value | Should -Match "Microsoft\.Network/applicationSecurityGroups"
          $module.properties.parameters.applicationSecurityGroupId.value | Should -Match "variables\('names'\)\.amplsAsg"
          # spokeNetwork.bicep names the ASG '<spoke VNet>-ampls-asg'.
          $failover.variables.names.amplsAsg | Should -Be "[format('vnet-defenstack-{0}-{1}-spoke-ampls-asg', parameters('environmentName'), variables('regionCode'))]"
          (Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'modules/spokeNetwork.bicep') -Raw) | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("var amplsAsgName = '`${vnetName}-ampls-asg'"))
      }

      It 'writes its records into the shared zones of the global resource group, re-pointing Azure Monitor to East US' {
          $module.properties.parameters.privateDnsZoneIds.value | Should -Match "Microsoft\.Network/privateDnsZones"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateDnsZoneIds.value | Should -Match "variables\('globalResourceGroupName'\)"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateDnsZoneIds.value | Should -Match "variables\('privateDnsZoneNames'\)"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover: East US blob private endpoint to the primary storage account (Phase 8)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $module = Get-FailoverModule 'failover-storage-private-endpoint'
          $endpointTemplate = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/failover/storagePrivateEndpoint.bicep'
          $endpoint = Get-TemplateResource -Template $endpointTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints' | Select-Object -First 1
          $zoneGroup = Get-TemplateResource -Template $endpointTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints/privateDnsZoneGroups' | Select-Object -First 1
          $stamp = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/regionStamp.bicep'
          $connectivity = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/privateConnectivity.bicep'
          $primaryEndpoint = Get-TemplateResource -Template $connectivity -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints' |
              Where-Object { $_.properties.privateLinkServiceConnections[0].properties.groupIds -contains 'blob' } | Select-Object -First 1
          $primaryZoneGroup = Get-TemplateResource -Template $connectivity -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints/privateDnsZoneGroups' |
              Where-Object { $_.properties.privateDnsZoneConfigs[0].name -eq 'blob' } | Select-Object -First 1
      }

      It 'defaults the account ID to the prod primary account, named as main.bicep and regionStamp.bicep name it' {
          $failover.parameters.primaryStorageAccountId.type | Should -Be 'string'
          $failover.parameters.primaryStorageAccountId.defaultValue | Should -BeExactly "[resourceId(subscription().subscriptionId, format('rg-defenstack-{0}-wus3', parameters('environmentName')), 'Microsoft.Storage/storageAccounts', format('stwus3{0}', uniqueString(subscription().id, parameters('environmentName'), 'westus3')))]"
          # The parts it hard-codes: the primary region, its code, the regional resource group and the account formula.
          $main.parameters.primaryLocation.defaultValue | Should -Be 'westus3'
          $main.variables.regionCodes.westus3 | Should -Be 'wus3'
          $main.variables.primaryResourceGroupName | Should -Be "[format('rg-defenstack-{0}-{1}', parameters('environmentName'), variables('primaryRegionCode'))]"
          $stamp.variables.nameSuffix | Should -Be "[uniqueString(subscription().id, parameters('environmentName'), parameters('location'))]"
          $stamp.variables.names.storageAccount | Should -Be "[format('st{0}{1}', parameters('regionCode'), variables('nameSuffix'))]"
      }

      It 'is named after the account with an -eus-pe suffix, distinct from its own -pe and the standby''s -secondary-pe endpoints' {
          $failover.variables.names.storagePrivateEndpoint | Should -BeExactly "[format('{0}-{1}-pe', last(split(parameters('primaryStorageAccountId'), '/')), variables('regionCode'))]"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointName.value | Should -Be "[variables('names').storagePrivateEndpoint]"
          $endpoint.name | Should -Be "[parameters('privateEndpointName')]"
          $primaryEndpoint.name | Should -Be "[format('{0}-pe', parameters('storageAccountName'))]"
          $secondary = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/storageSecondaryEndpoint.bicep'
          (Get-TemplateResource -Template $secondary -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints' | Select-Object -First 1).name | Should -Be "[format('{0}-secondary-pe', parameters('primaryStorageAccountName'))]"
      }

      It 'connects to the account''s blob sub-resource, as the primary stamp''s storage endpoint does' {
          $module.properties.parameters.storageAccountId.value | Should -Be "[parameters('primaryStorageAccountId')]"
          $connection = $endpoint.properties.privateLinkServiceConnections[0]
          $connection.name | Should -Be 'blob'
          @($connection.properties.groupIds) | Should -Be @('blob')
          $endpoint.apiVersion | Should -Be $primaryEndpoint.apiVersion
          $connection.name | Should -Be $primaryEndpoint.properties.privateLinkServiceConnections[0].name
          @($connection.properties.groupIds) | Should -Be @($primaryEndpoint.properties.privateLinkServiceConnections[0].properties.groupIds)
      }

      It 'sits in the East US spoke private-endpoint subnet' {
          $module.properties.parameters.location.value | Should -Be "[parameters('location')]"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointSubnetId.value | Should -Match "'private-endpoints'"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointSubnetId.value | Should -Match "variables\('resourceGroupName'\)"
          $module.properties.parameters.privateEndpointSubnetId.value | Should -Match "variables\('names'\)\.spokeVnet"
          $endpoint.properties.subnet.id | Should -Be "[parameters('privateEndpointSubnetId')]"
      }

      It 'registers in the shared privatelink.blob zone of the global resource group, overwriting the account''s record' {
          $module.properties.parameters.blobPrivateDnsZoneId.value | Should -BeExactly "[resourceId(subscription().subscriptionId, variables('globalResourceGroupName'), 'Microsoft.Network/privateDnsZones', variables('privateDnsZoneNames').blob)]"
          $zoneGroup.name | Should -Match "'default'"
          @($zoneGroup.properties.privateDnsZoneConfigs).Count | Should -Be 1
          $zoneGroup.properties.privateDnsZoneConfigs[0].name | Should -Be $primaryZoneGroup.properties.privateDnsZoneConfigs[0].name
          $zoneGroup.properties.privateDnsZoneConfigs[0].properties.privateDnsZoneId | Should -Be "[parameters('blobPrivateDnsZoneId')]"
          $zoneGroup.apiVersion | Should -Be $primaryZoneGroup.apiVersion
      }

      It 'grants no data role: the write role stays a manual, PIM-gated runbook step (ADR-020)' {
          @(Get-TemplateResource -Template $endpointTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Authorization/roleAssignments').Count | Should -Be 0
          $failoverJson | Should -Not -Match 'Microsoft\.Authorization/roleAssignments'
          $failoverJson | Should -Not -Match 'ba92f5b4-2d11-453d-a403-e96b0029c9fe'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover outputs (Phase 8)' {
      It 'reports what it deployed, or empty strings when disarmed' {
          foreach ($output in 'resourceGroupName', 'appServicePlanName', 'bastionName', 'monitorPrivateEndpointId', 'storagePrivateEndpointId') {
              $failover.outputs.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Contain $output
          }
          $failover.outputs.bastionName.value | Should -Match "^\[if\(parameters\('failoverActive'\)"
          $failover.outputs.monitorPrivateEndpointId.value | Should -Match "^\[if\(parameters\('failoverActive'\)"
          $failover.outputs.storagePrivateEndpointId.value | Should -Match "^\[if\(parameters\('failoverActive'\)"
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover parameter file (Phase 8)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $text = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'params/failover.bicepparam') -Raw
          [Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
          $built = (& bicep build-params (Get-RepoPath 'params/failover.bicepparam') --stdout) -join "`n" | ConvertFrom-Json
          $parameters = ($built.parametersJson | ConvertFrom-Json).parameters
      }

      It 'is armed for prod East US at the primary capacity' {
          $parameters.environmentName.value | Should -Be 'prod'
          $parameters.location.value | Should -Be 'eastus'
          $parameters.appServicePlanCapacity.value | Should -Be 3
          $parameters.failoverActive.value | Should -BeTrue
      }

      It 'leaves the primary storage account ID to the template default, which a parameter file cannot compute' {
          $parameters.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'primaryStorageAccountId'
          $text | Should -Match 'primaryStorageAccountId is not set here'
          $text | Should -Match '<account>-eus-pe'
      }

      It 'points the operator at runbook 08' {
          $text | Should -Match 'runbook 08'
      }
  }
  ```

  Replace `tests/Lint.Tests.ps1` (the drift test now covers all three root entry points):

  ```powershell
  BeforeDiscovery {
      $repoRoot = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
      # Match against each file's path relative to $repoRoot, not FullName: the repository can be
      # checked out under a directory that itself contains a path segment such as '.claude' (e.g. a
      # worktree under '<repo>\.claude\worktrees\<name>'), which would otherwise make every file look
      # excluded.
      $bicepFiles = Get-ChildItem -Path $repoRoot -Recurse -Filter '*.bicep' |
          ForEach-Object { @{ Name = $_.FullName.Substring($repoRoot.Length + 1); FullName = $_.FullName } } |
          Where-Object { $_.Name -notmatch '(^|[\\/])\.(git|claude|superpowers)([\\/]|$)' }
  }

  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force
  }

  Describe 'Repository linter configuration' {
      It 'has bicepconfig.json at the repository root' {
          Get-RepoPath 'bicepconfig.json' | Should -Exist
      }

      It 'raises security rules to error level' {
          $config = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'bicepconfig.json') -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json
          foreach ($rule in 'secure-parameter-default', 'outputs-should-not-contain-secrets', 'use-secure-value-for-secure-inputs', 'no-hardcoded-env-urls', 'secure-secrets-in-params') {
              $config.analyzers.core.rules.$rule.level | Should -Be 'error' -Because "$rule protects secrets and portability"
          }
      }

      It 'ignores local secret parameter overlays' {
          Get-Content (Get-RepoPath '.gitignore') | Should -Contain '*.local.bicepparam'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Bicep lint' {
      It '<Name> lints without errors' -ForEach $bicepFiles {
          $output = & bicep lint $FullName 2>&1
          $LASTEXITCODE | Should -Be 0 -Because ($output -join [Environment]::NewLine)
      }
  }

  Describe 'Generated ARM template' {
      # Every root entry point commits its build: main.json (pipeline), governance.json (operator, Phase 7) and
      # failover.json (operator during a declared outage, Phase 8).
      It '<_>.json matches a fresh build of <_>.bicep' -ForEach 'main', 'governance', 'failover' {
          [Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
          $expected = ((& bicep build (Get-RepoPath "$_.bicep") --stdout) -join "`n").Trim()
          $actual = ((Get-Content (Get-RepoPath "$_.json") -Raw -Encoding UTF8) -replace "`r`n", "`n").Trim()
          $actual | Should -BeExactly $expected -Because "run ""bicep build $_.bicep"" and commit $_.json"
      }
  }
  ```

  Replace `tests/MonitorPrivateLink.Tests.ps1`:

  ```powershell
  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force
      $scopeTemplate = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/monitorPrivateLinkScope.bicep'
      $scope = Get-TemplateResource -Template $scopeTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/privateLinkScopes' | Select-Object -First 1
      $scopedResources = Get-TemplateResource -Template $scopeTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Insights/privateLinkScopes/scopedResources' | Select-Object -First 1
      $endpointTemplate = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'modules/monitorPrivateEndpoint.bicep'
      $endpoint = Get-TemplateResource -Template $endpointTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints' | Select-Object -First 1
      $zoneGroup = Get-TemplateResource -Template $endpointTemplate -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints/privateDnsZoneGroups' | Select-Object -First 1
  }

  Describe 'Azure Monitor Private Link Scope (Phase 6)' {
      It 'is a global resource' {
          $scope.location | Should -Be 'global'
      }

      It 'accepts ingestion and queries from connected networks only through private links (PrivateOnly)' {
          $scope.properties.accessModeSettings.ingestionAccessMode | Should -Be 'PrivateOnly'
          $scope.properties.accessModeSettings.queryAccessMode | Should -Be 'PrivateOnly'
      }

      It 'pins the GA API version 2021-09-01' {
          $scope.apiVersion | Should -Be '2021-09-01'
          $scopedResources.apiVersion | Should -Be '2021-09-01'
      }

      It 'links one scoped resource per resource ID, named after the linked resource' {
          $scopedResources.copy.count | Should -Be "[length(parameters('scopedResourceIds'))]"
          $scopedResources.properties.linkedResourceId | Should -Be "[parameters('scopedResourceIds')[copyIndex()]]"
          $scopedResources.name | Should -Match "last\(split\(parameters\('scopedResourceIds'\)\[copyIndex\(\)\], '/'\)\)"
      }

      It 'outputs the scope ID and name' {
          $scopeTemplate.outputs.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Contain 'id'
          $scopeTemplate.outputs.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Contain 'name'
      }
  }

  Describe 'AMPLS private endpoint (Phase 6)' {
      It 'connects to the scope through the azuremonitor group' {
          $connection = $endpoint.properties.privateLinkServiceConnections[0]
          $connection.properties.privateLinkServiceId | Should -Be "[parameters('privateLinkScopeId')]"
          @($connection.properties.groupIds) | Should -Be @('azuremonitor')
      }

      It 'uses the existing <resource>-pe naming and the private-endpoint subnet passed in' {
          $endpoint.name | Should -Be "[parameters('privateEndpointName')]"
          $endpointTemplate.parameters.privateEndpointName.defaultValue | Should -Be "[format('{0}-pe', parameters('privateLinkScopeName'))]"
          $endpoint.properties.subnet.id | Should -Be "[parameters('privateEndpointSubnetId')]"
      }

      It 'takes an optional endpoint name, so failover.bicep can add a second endpoint in East US (Phase 8)' {
          $endpointTemplate.parameters.privateEndpointName.type | Should -Be 'string'
      }

      It 'keeps the Phase 6 endpoint byte-identical: main.bicep passes no name, so the default name (the scope name plus -pe) applies' {
          $main = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'main.bicep'
          $caller = Get-TemplateResourceBySymbol -Template $main -Symbol 'monitorPrivateEndpoint'
          $caller.properties.parameters.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'privateEndpointName'
          $caller.properties.parameters.privateLinkScopeName.value | Should -Be "[reference('monitorPrivateLinkScope').outputs.name.value]"
          $embedded = $caller.properties.template
          $embedded.parameters.privateEndpointName.defaultValue | Should -Be "[format('{0}-pe', parameters('privateLinkScopeName'))]"
          $embeddedEndpoint = Get-TemplateResource -Template $embedded -Type 'Microsoft.Network/privateEndpoints' | Select-Object -First 1
          $embeddedEndpoint.name | Should -Be "[parameters('privateEndpointName')]"
          # Every other property of the Phase 6 endpoint and its zone group is unchanged.
          @($embeddedEndpoint.properties.PSObject.Properties.Name) -join ',' | Should -Be 'privateLinkServiceConnections,subnet,applicationSecurityGroups'
          $embeddedEndpoint.properties.privateLinkServiceConnections[0].name | Should -Be 'azuremonitor'
          $embeddedEndpoint.properties.subnet.id | Should -Be "[parameters('privateEndpointSubnetId')]"
          $embeddedEndpoint.properties.applicationSecurityGroups[0].id | Should -Be "[parameters('applicationSecurityGroupId')]"
      }

      It 'joins the AMPLS application security group, the only destination the peer firewall may reach (final review I-1)' {
          $endpointTemplate.parameters.applicationSecurityGroupId.type | Should -Be 'string'
          @($endpoint.properties.applicationSecurityGroups).Count | Should -Be 1
          $endpoint.properties.applicationSecurityGroups[0].id | Should -Be "[parameters('applicationSecurityGroupId')]"
      }

      It 'registers records in the four Azure Monitor zones and the shared blob zone' {
          $zoneGroup.name | Should -Match "'default'\)\]$"
          $zoneIds = @($zoneGroup.properties.privateDnsZoneConfigs | ForEach-Object { $_.properties.privateDnsZoneId })
          $zoneIds.Count | Should -Be 5
          foreach ($key in 'monitor', 'oms', 'ods', 'agentsvc', 'blob') {
              $zoneIds | Should -Contain "[parameters('privateDnsZoneIds').$key]"
          }
      }
  }
  ```

  Replace `tests/Params.Tests.ps1`:

  ```powershell
  BeforeDiscovery {
      $repoRoot = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
      $paramFiles = Get-ChildItem -Path (Join-Path $repoRoot 'params') -Filter '*.bicepparam' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
          ForEach-Object { @{ Name = $_.Name; FullName = $_.FullName } }
  }

  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force

      function Get-BuiltParameters([string]$RelativePath) {
          [Console]::OutputEncoding = [System.Text.Encoding]::UTF8
          $built = (& bicep build-params (Get-RepoPath $RelativePath) --stdout) -join "`n" | ConvertFrom-Json
          ($built.parametersJson | ConvertFrom-Json).parameters
      }

      # True when every prefix in $Prefixes shares the first two octets of $Space (all ranges here are /16 spaces).
      function Test-InsideSixteen([string]$Space, [string[]]$Prefixes) {
          $root = ($Space -split '\.')[0..1] -join '.'
          -not ($Prefixes | Where-Object { -not $_.StartsWith("$root.") })
      }

      $dev = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/dev.bicepparam'
      $prod = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/prod.bicepparam'
  }

  Describe 'Committed parameter files' {
      It 'includes dev and prod parameter files' {
          Get-RepoPath 'params/dev.bicepparam' | Should -Exist
          Get-RepoPath 'params/prod.bicepparam' | Should -Exist
      }

      It 'binds dev and prod to main.bicep, governance to governance.bicep and failover to failover.bicep (Phases 7-8)' {
          Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'params/dev.bicepparam') -TotalCount 1 | Should -Be "using '../main.bicep'"
          Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'params/prod.bicepparam') -TotalCount 1 | Should -Be "using '../main.bicep'"
          Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'params/governance.bicepparam') -TotalCount 1 | Should -Be "using '../governance.bicep'"
          Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'params/failover.bicepparam') -TotalCount 1 | Should -Be "using '../failover.bicep'"
      }

      It '<Name> builds against its template' -ForEach $paramFiles {
          $output = & bicep build-params $FullName --stdout 2>&1
          $LASTEXITCODE | Should -Be 0 -Because ($output -join [Environment]::NewLine)
      }

      It '<Name> contains no az.getSecret references (secrets belong in *.local.bicepparam)' -ForEach $paramFiles {
          Get-Content $FullName -Raw | Should -Not -Match 'getSecret'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Environment topology' {
      It 'runs dev in the primary region only' {
          $dev.environmentName.value | Should -Be 'dev'
          $dev.deploySecondaryRegion.value | Should -BeExactly $false
      }

      It 'runs prod in both regions with a secondary address plan' {
          $prod.environmentName.value | Should -Be 'prod'
          $prod.deploySecondaryRegion.value | Should -BeExactly $true
          $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value | Should -Not -BeNullOrEmpty
      }
  }

  Describe 'Address plan' {
      It 'uses the spec ranges for prod (WUS3 hub 10.1/16, spoke 10.0/16; EUS hub 10.11/16, spoke 10.10/16)' {
          $prod.primaryAddressPlan.value.hubAddressSpace[0] | Should -Be '10.1.0.0/16'
          $prod.primaryAddressPlan.value.spokeAddressSpace[0] | Should -Be '10.0.0.0/16'
          $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value.hubAddressSpace[0] | Should -Be '10.11.0.0/16'
          $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value.spokeAddressSpace[0] | Should -Be '10.10.0.0/16'
      }

      It 'gives dev its own ranges so dev and prod never overlap' {
          $prodRanges = @($prod.primaryAddressPlan.value.hubAddressSpace + $prod.primaryAddressPlan.value.spokeAddressSpace +
              $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value.hubAddressSpace + $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value.spokeAddressSpace)
          foreach ($range in @($dev.primaryAddressPlan.value.hubAddressSpace + $dev.primaryAddressPlan.value.spokeAddressSpace)) {
              $prodRanges | Should -Not -Contain $range
          }
      }

      It 'keeps every subnet inside its VNet in <_> plans' -ForEach 'dev-primary', 'prod-primary', 'prod-secondary' {
          $plan = switch ($_) {
              'dev-primary' { $dev.primaryAddressPlan.value }
              'prod-primary' { $prod.primaryAddressPlan.value }
              'prod-secondary' { $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value }
          }
          Test-InsideSixteen $plan.hubAddressSpace[0] @($plan.firewallSubnetPrefix, $plan.bastionSubnetPrefix, $plan.gatewaySubnetPrefix) | Should -BeTrue
          Test-InsideSixteen $plan.spokeAddressSpace[0] @($plan.privateEndpointSubnetPrefix, $plan.appServiceIntegrationSubnetPrefix, $plan.managementSubnetPrefix) | Should -BeTrue
      }
  }

  Describe 'Admin access address plan (Phase 3)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $plans = @($dev.primaryAddressPlan.value, $prod.primaryAddressPlan.value, $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value)
      }

      It 'sizes the hub subnets for Azure (Bastion /26, GatewaySubnet /27) in <_> plans' -ForEach 'dev-primary', 'prod-primary', 'prod-secondary' {
          $plan = switch ($_) {
              'dev-primary' { $dev.primaryAddressPlan.value }
              'prod-primary' { $prod.primaryAddressPlan.value }
              'prod-secondary' { $prod.secondaryAddressPlan.value }
          }
          $plan.bastionSubnetPrefix | Should -Match '/26$'
          $plan.gatewaySubnetPrefix | Should -Match '/27$'
          @(@($plan.firewallSubnetPrefix, $plan.bastionSubnetPrefix, $plan.gatewaySubnetPrefix) | Select-Object -Unique) | Should -HaveCount 3
      }

      It 'uses the spec pool 172.16.200.0/24 for the prod primary region' {
          $prod.primaryAddressPlan.value.vpnClientAddressPool | Should -Be '172.16.200.0/24'
      }

      It 'gives every region and environment its own VPN client pool' {
          @($plans | ForEach-Object { $_.vpnClientAddressPool } | Select-Object -Unique) | Should -HaveCount 3
      }

      It 'keeps every VPN client pool outside every VNet (clients must never overlap Azure ranges)' {
          $vnetRoots = @($plans | ForEach-Object { @($_.hubAddressSpace) + @($_.spokeAddressSpace) } | ForEach-Object { ($_ -split '\.')[0..1] -join '.' })
          foreach ($plan in $plans) {
              $poolRoot = ($plan.vpnClientAddressPool -split '\.')[0..1] -join '.'
              $vnetRoots | Should -Not -Contain $poolRoot
          }
      }
  }

  Describe 'Front Door custom domain (Phase 4)' {
      It 'leaves the custom domain empty in <_> until the external DNS records exist (runbook 04)' -ForEach 'dev', 'prod' {
          $parameters = if ($_ -eq 'dev') { $dev } else { $prod }
          $parameters.customDomainHostName.value | Should -Be ''
      }
  }

  Describe 'Governance parameters (Phase 7)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $savedEmails = $env:SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS
          $governanceText = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'params/governance.bicepparam') -Raw
      }

      AfterAll {
          $env:SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS = $savedEmails
      }

      It 'commits no contact address; the operator sets SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS (runbook 07)' {
          $governanceText | Should -Not -Match '[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@[A-Za-z0-9-]+\.'
          $governanceText | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("readEnvironmentVariable('SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS', '')"))
      }

      It 'turns an unset SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS into an empty list, which the template refuses (minLength 1)' {
          $env:SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS = $null
          $parameters = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/governance.bicepparam'
          @($parameters.securityContactEmails.value).Count | Should -Be 0
      }

      It 'splits SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS on semicolons, trimming spaces and dropping empty entries' {
          $env:SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS = ' secops@example.com ; ;cloud-admins@example.com;'
          $parameters = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/governance.bicepparam'
          @($parameters.securityContactEmails.value) | Should -Be @('secops@example.com', 'cloud-admins@example.com')
      }

      It 'keeps the default 5000 GB malware scanning cap' {
          $parameters = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/governance.bicepparam'
          $parameters.malwareScanCapGbPerMonth.value | Should -Be 5000
      }

      It 'starts audit-first for dev and prod (ADR-027)' {
          $parameters = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/governance.bicepparam'
          $parameters.denyEnforcement.value | Should -Be 'DoNotEnforce'
          @($parameters.environments.value) -join ',' | Should -Be 'dev,prod'
      }

      It 'gives the legacy waiver a real yyyy-MM-dd expiry' {
          $parameters = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/governance.bicepparam'
          $parameters.legacyResourceGroupName.value | Should -Be 'defenStack'
          $expires = $parameters.legacyWaiverExpiresOn.value
          $expires | Should -Match '^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$'
          { [datetime]::ParseExact($expires, 'yyyy-MM-dd', [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture) } | Should -Not -Throw
      }

      It 'grants JIT requests to the same admin group each environment file signs in with (runbook 03)' {
          $parameters = Get-BuiltParameters 'params/governance.bicepparam'
          foreach ($pair in @(@{ Name = 'dev'; File = $dev }, @{ Name = 'prod'; File = $prod })) {
              $expected = if ($pair.File.PSObject.Properties.Name -contains 'adminGroupObjectId') { $pair.File.adminGroupObjectId.value } else { '' }
              $parameters.adminGroupObjectIds.value.($pair.Name) | Should -BeExactly $expected -Because $pair.Name
          }
      }

      It 'mirrors the environment parameter files in the governance region layout' {
          $governance = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'governance.bicep'
          $main = Get-BicepTemplate -RelativePath 'main.bicep'
          $layout = $governance.parameters.environmentRegions.defaultValue
          # Dev runs the primary only; prod adds the secondary. Neither file overrides the region defaults.
          $dev.deploySecondaryRegion.value | Should -BeExactly $false
          $prod.deploySecondaryRegion.value | Should -BeExactly $true
          foreach ($parameters in $dev, $prod) {
              $parameters.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'primaryLocation'
              $parameters.PSObject.Properties.Name | Should -Not -Contain 'secondaryLocation'
          }
          @($layout.dev) -join ',' | Should -Be $main.parameters.primaryLocation.defaultValue
          @($layout.prod) -join ',' | Should -Be "$($main.parameters.primaryLocation.defaultValue),$($main.parameters.secondaryLocation.defaultValue)"
      }
  }
  ```

  Replace `tests/Workflows.Tests.ps1`:

  ```powershell
  BeforeAll {
      Import-Module (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'Bicep.TestHelpers.psm1') -Force
      $ci = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath '.github/workflows/bicep-ci.yml') -Raw
      $deploy = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath '.github/workflows/deploy.yml') -Raw
  }

  Describe 'Workflows target the subscription scope' {
      It '<Name> uses az deployment sub, never az deployment group' -ForEach @(
          @{ Name = 'bicep-ci.yml'; Key = 'ci' },
          @{ Name = 'deploy.yml'; Key = 'deploy' }
      ) {
          $text = if ($Key -eq 'ci') { $ci } else { $deploy }
          $text | Should -Match 'az deployment sub '
          $text | Should -Not -Match 'az deployment group '
          $text | Should -Match 'DEPLOYMENT_LOCATION: westus3'
      }

      It '<Name> no longer reads the removed AZURE_RESOURCE_GROUP variable' -ForEach @(
          @{ Name = 'bicep-ci.yml'; Key = 'ci' },
          @{ Name = 'deploy.yml'; Key = 'deploy' }
      ) {
          $text = if ($Key -eq 'ci') { $ci } else { $deploy }
          $text | Should -Not -Match 'AZURE_RESOURCE_GROUP'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Deploy workflow gating' {
      It 'offers dev and prod and deploys only from main' {
          $deploy | Should -Match 'options: \[dev, prod\]'
          $deploy | Should -Match "if: github\.ref == 'refs/heads/main'"
          $deploy | Should -Match "environment: \$\{\{ inputs\.environment \|\| 'dev' \}\}"
      }

      It 'runs validate and what-if before create' {
          $validate = $deploy.IndexOf('az deployment sub validate')
          $whatIf = $deploy.IndexOf('az deployment sub what-if')
          $create = $deploy.IndexOf('az deployment sub create')
          $validate | Should -BeGreaterThan -1
          $whatIf | Should -BeGreaterThan $validate
          $create | Should -BeGreaterThan $whatIf
      }
  }

  Describe 'Deploy workflow plan/apply split (Phase 2)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $planStart = $deploy.IndexOf('  plan:')
          $applyStart = $deploy.IndexOf('  apply:')
          $planJob = $deploy.Substring($planStart, $applyStart - $planStart)
          $applyJob = $deploy.Substring($applyStart)
      }

      It 'has a plan job before an apply job' {
          $planStart | Should -BeGreaterThan -1
          $applyStart | Should -BeGreaterThan $planStart
      }

      It 'runs validate and what-if in the plan job, gated to prod for prod and dev-plan otherwise' {
          $planJob | Should -Match "environment: \$\{\{ inputs\.environment == 'prod' && 'prod' \|\| 'dev-plan' \}\}"
          $planJob | Should -Match 'az deployment sub validate'
          $planJob | Should -Match 'az deployment sub what-if'
          $planJob | Should -Not -Match 'az deployment sub create'
      }

      It 'creates only in the apply job, which waits for plan and runs in the reviewer-gated {env} environment' {
          $applyJob | Should -Match 'needs: plan'
          $applyJob | Should -Match "environment: \$\{\{ inputs\.environment \|\| 'dev' \}\}\s"
          $applyJob | Should -Match 'az deployment sub create'
          $applyJob | Should -Not -Match 'az deployment sub what-if'
      }

      It 'runs both jobs from main only' {
          ([regex]::Matches($deploy, "if: github\.ref == 'refs/heads/main'")).Count | Should -Be 2
      }

      It 'runs the PR what-if in dev-plan' {
          $ci | Should -Match 'environment: dev-plan'
      }

      It 'has no prod-plan environment anywhere' {
          $deploy | Should -Not -Match 'prod-plan'
      }

      It 'retains the what-if artifact for 14 days' {
          $planJob | Should -Match 'retention-days: 14'
      }

      It 'overwrites the what-if artifact so a re-run of a failed plan job does not fail on an existing artifact name (Final review D.4)' {
          $planJob | Should -Match 'overwrite: true'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Front Door private endpoint approval (Phase 4)' {
      It 'approves Front Door connections after the deployment, from the deployment outputs' {
          $create = $deploy.IndexOf('az deployment sub create')
          $approve = $deploy.IndexOf('./scripts/Approve-FrontDoorPrivateEndpoints.ps1')
          $approve | Should -BeGreaterThan $create
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('--query properties.outputs --output json'))
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('az deployment sub show --name "gh-${{ github.run_id }}-${{ github.run_attempt }}"'))
      }

      It 'fails the job when the outputs cannot be read' {
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("throw 'Could not read appServiceIds from the deployment outputs.'"))
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("throw 'Could not read frontDoorProfileName from the deployment outputs.'"))
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("throw 'Could not read globalResourceGroupName from the deployment outputs.'"))
      }

      It 'passes the Front Door profile and global resource group names to the approval script (final review A)' {
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('-AppServiceId $ids -FrontDoorProfileName $profileName -FrontDoorResourceGroupName $resourceGroupName'))
      }

      It 'refreshes the Azure login right before the approval step, since the wait can outlast the first token' {
          $create = $deploy.IndexOf('az deployment sub create')
          $refresh = $deploy.IndexOf('Refresh Azure login before approval')
          $approve = $deploy.IndexOf('Approve Front Door private endpoint connections')
          $refresh | Should -BeGreaterThan $create
          $approve | Should -BeGreaterThan $refresh

          $applyStart = $deploy.IndexOf('  apply:')
          $applyJob = $deploy.Substring($applyStart)
          ([regex]::Matches($applyJob, 'uses: azure/login@v2')).Count | Should -Be 2
      }
  }

  Describe 'Alert email from the ALERT_EMAIL environment variable (Phase 6)' {
      BeforeAll {
          $applyStart = $deploy.IndexOf('  apply:')
          $applyJob = $deploy.Substring($applyStart)
          $planJob = $deploy.Substring(0, $applyStart)
          $alertEmailArgument = '--parameters alertEmail="$ALERT_EMAIL"'
          $alertEmailVariable = 'ALERT_EMAIL: ${{ vars.ALERT_EMAIL }}'
      }

      It 'passes alertEmail from vars.ALERT_EMAIL to validate, what-if and create' {
          ([regex]::Matches($planJob, [regex]::Escape($alertEmailArgument))).Count | Should -Be 2
          ([regex]::Matches($applyJob, [regex]::Escape($alertEmailArgument))).Count | Should -Be 1
          ([regex]::Matches($deploy, [regex]::Escape($alertEmailVariable))).Count | Should -Be 5
      }

      It 'fails the prod plan first, before checkout and sign-in, when ALERT_EMAIL is empty (final review M-7)' {
          $planSteps = $planJob.Substring($planJob.IndexOf('  plan:'))
          $check = $planSteps.IndexOf('- name: Require ALERT_EMAIL for prod')
          $check | Should -BeGreaterThan -1
          $check | Should -BeLessThan $planSteps.IndexOf('- uses: actions/checkout@v4')
          $check | Should -BeLessThan $planSteps.IndexOf('uses: azure/login@v2')
          $planSteps.Substring($planSteps.IndexOf('steps:')).TrimStart() | Should -Match '^steps:\s+(#[^\n]*\n\s+)*- name: Require ALERT_EMAIL for prod'
          $step = $planSteps.Substring($check, $planSteps.IndexOf('- uses: actions/checkout@v4') - $check)
          $step | Should -Match "if: env\.TARGET_ENV == 'prod'"
          $step | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape($alertEmailVariable))
          $step | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('if [ -z "$ALERT_EMAIL" ]; then'))
          $step | Should -Match 'exit 1'
          # The address reaches the script through env:, never interpolated into run:.
          $step.Substring($step.IndexOf('run:')) | Should -Not -Match '\$\{\{'
      }

      It 'passes alertEmail to the pull request what-if, so it shows no spurious receiver change' {
          $ci | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape($alertEmailArgument))
          $ci | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape($alertEmailVariable))
      }

      It 'fails the prod apply before signing in to Azure when ALERT_EMAIL is empty' {
          $check = $applyJob.IndexOf('- name: Require ALERT_EMAIL for prod')
          $check | Should -BeGreaterThan -1
          $check | Should -BeLessThan $applyJob.IndexOf('uses: azure/login@v2')
          $check | Should -BeLessThan $applyJob.IndexOf('az deployment sub create')
          $step = $applyJob.Substring($check, $applyJob.IndexOf('- uses: actions/checkout@v4') - $check)
          $step | Should -Match "if: env\.TARGET_ENV == 'prod'"
          $step | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('if [ -z "$ALERT_EMAIL" ]; then'))
          $step | Should -Match 'exit 1'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Governance stays outside the pipeline (Phase 7)' {
      It 'does not redeploy the workload when only governance files change (a redeploy resets the JIT NSG rules, ADR-028)' {
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("      - 'modules/**'"))
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("      - '!modules/governance/**'"))
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("      - '!params/governance.bicepparam'"))
          # GitHub applies path filters in order, so each exclusion must follow the pattern it narrows.
          $deploy.IndexOf("'!modules/governance/**'") | Should -BeGreaterThan $deploy.IndexOf("'modules/**'")
          $deploy.IndexOf("'!params/governance.bicepparam'") | Should -BeGreaterThan $deploy.IndexOf("'params/**'")
      }

      It 'deploys only the environment parameter files, never governance' {
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('--parameters "params/${TARGET_ENV}.bicepparam"'))
          $deploy | Should -Match 'options: \[dev, prod\]'
      }

      It 'still runs PSRule over every parameter file, governance included, with a placeholder contact list' {
          $ci | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('Assert-PSRule -InputPath params/'))
          $psRuleStep = $ci.Substring($ci.IndexOf('- name: PSRule for Azure'))
          $psRuleStep | Should -Match 'SECURITY_CONTACT_EMAILS: security-contact@example\.com'
      }
  }

  Describe 'Failover stays outside the pipeline (Phase 8)' {
      It 'does not redeploy the workload when only failover files change (a redeploy scales the East US plan back to 1, ADR-032)' {
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("      - '!modules/failover/**'"))
          $deploy | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape("      - '!params/failover.bicepparam'"))
          # GitHub applies path filters in order, so each exclusion must follow the pattern it narrows.
          $deploy.IndexOf("'!modules/failover/**'") | Should -BeGreaterThan $deploy.IndexOf("'modules/**'")
          $deploy.IndexOf("'!params/failover.bicepparam'") | Should -BeGreaterThan $deploy.IndexOf("'params/**'")
      }

      It 'never triggers on failover.bicep, which no include pattern matches' {
          $paths = $deploy.Substring($deploy.IndexOf('paths:'), $deploy.IndexOf('workflow_dispatch:') - $deploy.IndexOf('paths:'))
          $paths | Should -Not -Match "- 'failover\.bicep'"
          $paths | Should -Not -Match "- '\*\.bicep'"
          $paths | Should -Match "- 'main\.bicep'"
      }

      It 'runs PSRule over params/, which includes the failover parameter file' {
          $ci | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('Assert-PSRule -InputPath params/'))
          Get-RepoPath 'params/failover.bicepparam' | Should -Exist
      }

      It 'builds, lints and drift-checks failover.bicep through the test suite that CI runs' {
          $ci | Should -Match ([regex]::Escape('./tests/Invoke-Tests.ps1 -CI'))
          $lint = Get-Content (Get-RepoPath 'tests/Lint.Tests.ps1') -Raw
          $lint | Should -Match "-ForEach 'main', 'governance', 'failover'"
      }
  }
  ```
